34509 bytes · sha256 `736f7f9aeeedb6f2fdd0a5e383da4e65e7abb01986ed2b97ca70b782ecf178bb`

```ts
import gsap from 'gsap';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { sfx, unlockAudio } from './game/audio';
import { BANANA_TIME, COUNTDOWN, ENERGY_TIME, GAP_LIMIT, ITEM_SLOTS, MUSCAT_TIME, MAX_V, PACE_COLORS, PACE_LABELS } from './game/constants';
import { SteerInput } from './game/input';
import { computeStats } from './game/stats';
import { createSim, hasGrip, isEnergized, isSprinting, isTurbo, prepareCourse, useItem, type ItemKind, stepSim, type SimState } from './game/sim';
import { sampleAt, worldAt, type Course } from './route/course';
import { parseGpx } from './route/gpx';
import { formatPace, formatTime } from './route/pace';
import { SAMPLE_RUNS } from './route/samples';
import type { Run } from './route/types';
import { Minimap } from './ui/minimap';
import { CHARACTERS, createRunner, renderThumbnails, type CharacterId } from './world/character';
import { buildCoins, type CoinsView } from './world/coins';
import { buildFruits, type FruitsView } from './world/fruits';
import { buildGels, type GelsView } from './world/gels';
import { buildNature, type NatureView } from './world/nature';
import { buildRoad, type RoadView } from './world/road';
import { createWorld, followSun } from './world/scene';

type Screen = 'home' | 'select' | 'stats' | 'preview' | 'character' | 'play' | 'result' | 'over';
type CamMode = 'portrait' | 'overview' | 'chase' | 'finish';
type PlayPhase = 'intro' | 'countdown' | 'running' | 'ending';

const $ = <T extends HTMLElement = HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;

// ---------- world ----------
const world = createWorld($<HTMLCanvasElement>('gl'));
const { scene, camera } = world;
const fog = scene.fog as THREE.Fog;
const input = new SteerInput($('gl'));
/** The player's main runner: shown on Home and preselected for every race. */
const CHAR_KEY = 'pace-runner:character';
function loadCharacter(): CharacterId {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(CHAR_KEY);
    if (CHARACTERS.some((c) => c.id === v)) return v as CharacterId;
  } catch {
    /* storage unavailable */
  }
  return CHARACTERS[0].id;
}
let mainId = loadCharacter();
/** runner currently on screen (the race pick, or the main runner in menus) */
let characterId = mainId;
let runner = createRunner({ kind: characterId });
let ghost = createRunner({ ghost: true, kind: characterId });
scene.add(runner.group, ghost.group);
if ('ontouchstart' in window) document.body.classList.add('touch');

const hudMap = new Minimap($<HTMLCanvasElement>('hud-map'), { line: 3.5, pad: 12, streets: 0.6 });
const previewMap = new Minimap($<HTMLCanvasElement>('pv-map'), { line: 5, pad: 26, glow: true, streets: 1, pin: true });

const runs: Run[] = [];
const courses = new Map<string, Course>();
const courseFor = (run: Run) => {
  let c = courses.get(run.id);
  if (!c) courses.set(run.id, (c = prepareCourse(run)));
  return c;
};

let course: Course | null = null;
let road: RoadView | null = null;
let nature: NatureView | null = null;
let coinsView: CoinsView | null = null;
let gelsView: GelsView | null = null;
let fruitsView: FruitsView | null = null;

function dispose(obj: THREE.Object3D) {
  obj.traverse((o) => {
    if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh) {
      o.geometry.dispose();
      for (const m of Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]) {
        (m as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).map?.dispose();
        m.dispose();
      }
    }
  });
  obj.removeFromParent();
}

/** Make `id` the main runner (persisted) and show it. */
function setMain(id: CharacterId) {
  mainId = id;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(CHAR_KEY, id);
  } catch {
    /* storage unavailable */
  }
  setCharacter(id);
  renderPickers();
}

/** Show `id` as the current runner. The race pick doesn't change the main runner. */
function setCharacter(id: CharacterId) {
  if (id === characterId) return;
  characterId = id;
  const { position, rotation } = runner.group;
  const ghostVisible = ghost.group.visible;
  const gp = ghost.group.position.clone();
  const gr = ghost.group.rotation.y;
  dispose(runner.group);
  dispose(ghost.group);
  runner = createRunner({ kind: id });
  ghost = createRunner({ ghost: true, kind: id });
  runner.group.position.copy(position);
  runner.group.rotation.y = rotation.y;
  ghost.group.position.copy(gp);
  ghost.group.rotation.y = gr;
  ghost.group.visible = ghostVisible;
  scene.add(runner.group, ghost.group);
  gsap.fromTo(runner.group.scale, { x: 0.6, y: 1.3, z: 0.6 }, { x: 1, y: 1, z: 1, duration: 0.6, ease: 'elastic.out(1, 0.45)' });
  renderPickers();
}

// ---------- character picker ----------
const thumbs = renderThumbnails();

function renderPickers() {
  for (const el of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.char-picker')) {
    el.innerHTML = '';
    for (const c of CHARACTERS) {
      // the main-runner picker (My Stats) edits mainId; the race picker edits the current runner
      const isMainPicker = el.classList.contains('main-picker');
      const selected = isMainPicker ? mainId : characterId;
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'char-option' + (c.id === selected ? ' selected' : '') + (c.id === mainId ? ' is-main' : '');
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(c.id === selected));
      b.title = c.blurb;
      b.innerHTML = '<img alt="" /><span></span><small></small>';
      b.querySelector('img')!.src = thumbs[c.id];
      b.querySelector('span')!.textContent = c.name;
      b.querySelector('small')!.textContent = c.blurb;
      b.addEventListener('click', () => (isMainPicker ? setMain(c.id) : setCharacter(c.id)));
      el.appendChild(b);
    }
  }
}
renderPickers();

function loadCourse(c: Course) {
  if (course === c) return;
  if (road) dispose(road.group);
  if (nature) dispose(nature.group);
  if (coinsView) dispose(coinsView.group);
  if (gelsView) dispose(gelsView.group);
  if (fruitsView) dispose(fruitsView.group);
  course = c;
  road = buildRoad(c);
  nature = buildNature(c);
  coinsView = buildCoins(c);
  gelsView = buildGels(c);
  fruitsView = buildFruits(c);
  scene.add(road.group, nature.group, coinsView.group, gelsView.group, fruitsView.group);
  hudMap.setCourse(c);
  placeRunner(0, 0, 0);
  ghost.group.visible = false;
}

// ---------- state ----------
let screen: Screen = 'home';
let camMode: CamMode = 'portrait';
let phase: PlayPhase = 'intro';
let phaseTime = 0;
let sim: SimState = createSim();
let postDist = 0;
let postV = 0;
let shake = 0;
let clockTime = 0;
let camLerp = 6;

const camPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 4, 10);
const camLook = new THREE.Vector3();
const tmpPos = new THREE.Vector3();
const tmpLook = new THREE.Vector3();
/** smoothed chase-camera heading (x, z) */
const camHeading = new THREE.Vector2(0, 1);

function placeRunner(dist: number, lateral: number, yaw: number) {
  if (!course) return;
  const p = worldAt(course, dist, lateral);
  runner.group.position.set(p.x, 0, p.z);
  runner.group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(p.tx, p.tz) + yaw;
}

// ---------- screens ----------
const SCREENS: Record<Screen, string> = {
  home: 'screen-home',
  select: 'screen-select',
  preview: 'screen-preview',
  character: 'screen-character',
  stats: 'screen-stats',
  play: 'hud',
  result: 'screen-result',
  over: 'screen-over',
};

function show(next: Screen) {
  screen = next;
  for (const [key, id] of Object.entries(SCREENS)) {
    const el = $(id);
    const on = key === next;
    el.classList.toggle('active', on);
    if (on) gsap.fromTo(el, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'power2.out' });
  }
  const panel = $(SCREENS[next]).querySelector('.panel, .home, .routes-head');
  if (panel) gsap.fromTo(panel, { y: 24, scale: 0.97 }, { y: 0, scale: 1, duration: 0.5, ease: 'back.out(1.6)' });
}

let toastTimer = 0;
function toast(msg: string) {
  const el = $('toast');
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = window.setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 2600);
}

const bestKey = (id: string) => `pace-runner:best:${id}`;
function getBest(id: string): number | null {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(bestKey(id));
    return v ? Number(v) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function setBest(id: string, t: number) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(bestKey(id), String(t));
  } catch {
    /* storage unavailable — best time just won't persist */
  }
}

const coinKey = (id: string) => `pace-runner:coins:${id}`;
function getBestCoins(id: string): number {
  try {
    return Number(localStorage.getItem(coinKey(id)) ?? 0);
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}
function setBestCoins(id: string, n: number) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(coinKey(id), String(n));
  } catch {
    /* storage unavailable */
  }
}

const fmtClock = (sec: number) => {
  const s = Math.round(sec);
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  const r = String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  return h ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${r}` : `${m}:${r}`;
};

// ---------- home ----------
$('btn-sync').addEventListener('click', () => {
  unlockAudio();
  const btn = $<HTMLButtonElement>('btn-sync');
  btn.disabled = true;
  $('sync-status').textContent = 'Loading your runs…';
  // MVP: phone sync is simulated with bundled sample runs; GPX upload covers real data.
  setTimeout(() => {
    ensureRuns();
    btn.disabled = false;
    $('sync-status').textContent = '';
    openSelect();
  }, 700);
});

// ---------- select ----------
/** Routes grid: each finished run is a collectible — big route art, stats and reward badges. */
function openSelect() {
  setCharacter(mainId);
  show('select');
  camMode = 'portrait';
  ghost.group.visible = false;
  const list = $('run-list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  let totalKm = 0;
  let played = 0;
  runs.forEach((run, idx) => {
    let c: Course;
    try {
      c = courseFor(run);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    totalKm += c.realDistanceM / 1000;
    const best = getBest(run.id);
    if (best) played++;
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = `route-card tint-${idx % 4}`;
    li.tabIndex = 0;
    li.innerHTML = `
      <div class="route-art"><canvas></canvas><span class="route-km"></span></div>
      <div class="route-info">
        <h3></h3>
        <p class="route-date"></p>
        <div class="route-stats"><span class="t"></span><span class="p"></span></div>
        <div class="route-badges"></div>
      </div>`;
    li.querySelector('.route-km')!.textContent = `${(c.realDistanceM / 1000).toFixed(1)} km`;
    li.querySelector('h3')!.textContent = run.name;
    li.querySelector('.route-date')!.textContent = run.date;
    li.querySelector('.route-stats .t')!.textContent = fmtClock(c.realDurationSec);
    li.querySelector('.route-stats .p')!.textContent = `${formatPace(c.avgPace)}/km`;
    const badges = li.querySelector('.route-badges')!;
    const badge = (cls: string, text: string) => {
      const b = document.createElement('span');
      b.className = `badge ${cls}`;
      b.textContent = text;
      badges.appendChild(b);
    };
    if (best) badge('best', `★ ${formatTime(best)}`);
    else badge('new', 'New');
    badge('coin', `${best ? getBestCoins(run.id) : 0}/${c.coins.length}`);
    if (c.gels.length) badge('gel', `${c.gels.length} gel${c.gels.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
    const open = () => openPreview(run);
    li.addEventListener('click', open);
    li.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && open());
    list.appendChild(li);
    new Minimap(li.querySelector('canvas')!, { line: 5, pad: 20, padTop: 44, glow: true, streets: 1, pin: true }).setCourse(c);
  });

  // "add a run" card opens the GPX picker
  const add = document.createElement('li');
  add.className = 'route-card add';
  add.innerHTML = `<label for="gpx-input"><span class="plus">+</span><b>Add a run</b><small>Upload a GPX file</small></label>`;
  list.appendChild(add);

  $('routes-sum').textContent = `${runs.length} routes · ${totalKm.toFixed(1)} km · ${played} raced`;
  gsap.fromTo('.route-card', { y: 18, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 0.45, stagger: 0.05, ease: 'power2.out' });
}

$('btn-select-back').addEventListener('click', goHome);
$('btn-routes-stats').addEventListener('click', () => openStats('select'));

function goHome() {
  setCharacter(mainId);
  show('home');
  camMode = 'portrait';
}

// ---------- my stats ----------
let statsBack: Screen = 'home';

function ensureRuns() {
  if (!runs.length) runs.push(...SAMPLE_RUNS);
}

function openStats(from: Screen) {
  ensureRuns();
  statsBack = from;
  setCharacter(mainId);
  show('stats');
  camMode = 'portrait';
  ghost.group.visible = false;
  renderPickers();
  const courses = runs.flatMap((r) => {
    try {
      return [courseFor(r)];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });
  const st = computeStats(courses, getBest, getBestCoins);
  const cell = (k: string, v: string, sub = '') =>
    `<div><dt>${k}</dt><dd>${v}</dd>${sub ? `<small>${sub}</small>` : ''}</div>`;
  const esc = (t: string) => t.replace(/[&<>"]/g, (ch) => `&#${ch.charCodeAt(0)};`);
  $('st-summary').innerHTML = [
    cell('Total distance', `${st.totalKm.toFixed(1)} km`),
    cell('Total time', fmtClock(st.totalSec)),
    cell('Runs', String(st.runs)),
    cell('Avg pace', `${formatPace(st.avgPace)}/km`),
    cell('Longest run', st.longest ? `${st.longest.km.toFixed(1)} km` : '—', st.longest ? esc(st.longest.name) : ''),
    cell('Fastest run', st.fastest ? `${formatPace(st.fastest.pace)}/km` : '—', st.fastest ? esc(st.fastest.name) : ''),
  ].join('');
  $('st-pace-bar').innerHTML = st.paceShare
    .map((share, b) => `<i style="flex:${share};background:${PACE_COLORS[b]}" title="${PACE_LABELS[b]}"></i>`)
    .join('');
  $('st-pace-legend').innerHTML = st.paceShare
    .map((share, b) => `<li><i style="background:${PACE_COLORS[b]}"></i>${PACE_LABELS[b]} <b>${Math.round(share * 100)}%</b></li>`)
    .join('');
  const medalIcon = { gold: '🥇', silver: '🥈', bronze: '🥉' } as const;
  $('st-bests').innerHTML = st.bests
    .map(
      (b) => `<li>
        <span class="medal">${b.medal ? medalIcon[b.medal] : '·'}</span>
        <span class="name">${esc(b.name)}<small>${b.km.toFixed(1)} km</small></span>
        <span class="time">${b.bestTime === null ? 'Not raced' : formatTime(b.bestTime)}</span>
        <span class="coins">$ ${b.bestCoins}/${b.totalCoins}</span>
      </li>`,
    )
    .join('');
}

$('btn-stats').addEventListener('click', () => {
  unlockAudio();
  openStats('home');
});
$('btn-stats-back').addEventListener('click', () => (statsBack === 'select' ? openSelect() : goHome()));

$<HTMLInputElement>('gpx-input').addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
  const inputEl = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
  const file = inputEl.files?.[0];
  inputEl.value = '';
  if (!file) return;
  try {
    const run = parseGpx(await file.text(), file.name.replace(/\.gpx$/i, ''));
    courseFor(run);
    runs.unshift(run);
    toast(`Added '${run.name}'`);
    openSelect();
  } catch (err) {
    toast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not read that GPX file');
  }
});

// ---------- preview ----------
let selectedRun: Run | null = null;

function openPreview(run: Run) {
  selectedRun = run;
  const c = courseFor(run);
  loadCourse(c);
  show('preview');
  camMode = 'overview';
  $('pv-date').textContent = run.date;
  $('pv-name').textContent = run.name;
  $('pv-stats').innerHTML = '';
  const stats: [string, string][] = [
    ['Distance', `${(c.realDistanceM / 1000).toFixed(2)}km`],
    ['Real time', fmtClock(c.realDurationSec)],
    ['Avg pace', formatPace(c.avgPace)],
  ];
  for (const [k, v] of stats) {
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.innerHTML = '<dt></dt><dd></dd>';
    d.querySelector('dt')!.textContent = k;
    d.querySelector('dd')!.textContent = v;
    $('pv-stats').appendChild(d);
  }
  const legend = $('pv-legend');
  legend.innerHTML = '';
  const step = (c.paceMax - c.paceMin) / 4;
  PACE_COLORS.forEach((color, b) => {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.innerHTML = `<i></i><span></span><small></small>`;
    li.querySelector('i')!.style.background = color;
    li.querySelector('span')!.textContent = PACE_LABELS[b];
    li.querySelector('small')!.textContent = `${formatPace(c.paceMin + step * b)}–${formatPace(c.paceMin + step * (b + 1))}`;
    legend.appendChild(li);
  });
  previewMap.setCourse(c);
}

$('btn-preview-back').addEventListener('click', openSelect);
$('btn-choose-course').addEventListener('click', openCharacter);

// ---------- character select ----------
function openCharacter() {
  if (!selectedRun) return;
  unlockAudio();
  setCharacter(mainId);
  show('character');
  camMode = 'portrait';
  ghost.group.visible = false;
  placeRunner(0, 0, 0);
  $('ch-course').textContent = selectedRun.name;
  renderPickers();
}

$('btn-character-back').addEventListener('click', () => selectedRun && openPreview(selectedRun));
$('btn-start').addEventListener('click', () => {
  unlockAudio();
  startRun();
});

// ---------- play ----------
function startRun() {
  if (!course) return;
  sim = createSim();
  input.reset();
  postDist = 0;
  postV = 0;
  phase = 'intro';
  phaseTime = 0;
  camLerp = 2.2;
  camMode = 'chase';
  placeRunner(0, 0, 0);
  if (course) {
    const p0 = worldAt(course, 0, 0);
    camHeading.set(p0.tx, p0.tz);
  }
  ghost.group.visible = true;
  show('play');
  hudMap.layout();
  renderItemSlots();
  $('countdown').textContent = '';
}

function quitRun() {
  if (screen === 'play') openSelect();
}
$('btn-quit').addEventListener('click', quitRun);
window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.code === 'Escape') quitRun();
  if (screen === 'play' && phase === 'running' && !e.repeat) {
    const slot = e.code === 'Space' ? 0 : ['Digit1', 'Digit2', 'Digit3'].indexOf(e.code);
    if (slot >= 0) {
      e.preventDefault();
      activateItem(slot);
    }
  }
  if ((e.code === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') && (screen === 'result' || screen === 'over')) startRun();
});

for (const id of ['btn-rs-retry', 'btn-ov-retry']) $(id).addEventListener('click', startRun);
for (const id of ['btn-rs-select', 'btn-ov-select']) $(id).addEventListener('click', openSelect);

function flash(kind: 'turbo' | 'offroad' | 'gel') {
  const el = $('flash');
  el.classList.toggle('offroad', kind === 'offroad');
  el.classList.toggle('gel', kind === 'gel');
  gsap.fromTo(el, { opacity: 1 }, { opacity: 0, duration: kind === 'turbo' ? 0.9 : 0.5, ease: 'power2.out' });
}

function bumpCoins() {
  gsap.fromTo('#hud-coins-chip', { scale: 1.25 }, { scale: 1, duration: 0.3, ease: 'back.out(3)' });
}

// ---------- carried items ----------
const ITEM_INFO: Record<ItemKind, { name: string; banner: string; icon: string }> = {
  gel: {
    name: 'Energy gel',
    banner: 'ENERGY GEL!',
    icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 32"><rect x="3" y="7" width="18" height="23" rx="5" fill="#e8414f"/><rect x="7" y="2" width="10" height="7" rx="3" fill="#f8a9b4"/><path d="M13 12l-4 7h3l-1 6 5-8h-3z" fill="#fff"/></svg>',
  },
  banana: {
    name: 'Banana',
    banner: 'BANANA GRIP!',
    icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M5 9c2 10 10 16 22 13-2 4-9 6-15 3S3 16 5 9z" fill="#ffd84d" stroke="#e0a81e" stroke-width="1.5"/><circle cx="5" cy="9" r="2" fill="#8a5a2b"/></svg>',
  },
  muscat: {
    name: 'Shine muscat',
    banner: 'SHINE MUSCAT SPRINT!',
    icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><circle cx="16" cy="18" r="11" fill="#b8e86a" stroke="#86b83a" stroke-width="1.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="3" fill="#f4ffe0"/><path d="M16 7l2-5" stroke="#7a9a3a" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>',
  },
};

function renderItemSlots(popLast = false) {
  const wrap = $('hud-items');
  wrap.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_SLOTS; i++) {
    const kind = sim.inventory[i];
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'item-slot' + (kind ? ' full' : '');
    b.disabled = !kind;
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', kind ? `Use ${ITEM_INFO[kind].name}` : 'Empty slot');
    b.innerHTML = `${kind ? ITEM_INFO[kind].icon : ''}<kbd>${i === 0 ? 'Space' : i + 1}</kbd>`;
    b.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      activateItem(i);
    });
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  }
  if (popLast && sim.inventory.length) {
    gsap.fromTo(wrap.children[sim.inventory.length - 1], { scale: 1.35 }, { scale: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'back.out(3)' });
  }
}

function activateItem(slot: number) {
  const kind = useItem(sim, slot);
  if (!kind) return;
  renderItemSlots();
  sfx.gel();
  flash('gel');
  countdownText(ITEM_INFO[kind].banner, true);
}

let bagFullAt = -10;
function showBagFull() {
  if (clockTime - bagFullAt < 1.5) return;
  bagFullAt = clockTime;
  const el = $('hud-bag');
  el.textContent = 'Bag full — use an item!';
  gsap.fromTo(el, { opacity: 1, y: 0 }, { opacity: 0, y: -10, duration: 1.4, delay: 0.6 });
}

function countdownText(text: string, banner = false) {
  const el = $('countdown');
  el.textContent = text;
  el.classList.toggle('banner', banner);
  gsap.killTweensOf(el);
  gsap.fromTo(el, { scale: 1.6, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1, opacity: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'back.out(2)' });
  if (text === 'GO!' || banner) gsap.to(el, { opacity: 0, scale: 1.2, duration: 0.4, delay: banner ? 1.1 : 0.5 });
}

function updatePlay(dt: number) {
  if (!course) return;
  phaseTime += dt;
  const steer = input.update(dt);

  if (phase === 'intro') {
    runner.idle(clockTime);
    if (phaseTime > 1.3) {
      phase = 'countdown';
      phaseTime = 0;
      countdownText(String(COUNTDOWN));
      sfx.tick();
    }
  } else if (phase === 'countdown') {
    runner.idle(clockTime);
    const before = Math.ceil(COUNTDOWN - (phaseTime - dt));
    const now = Math.ceil(COUNTDOWN - phaseTime);
    if (now !== before && now > 0) {
      countdownText(String(now));
      sfx.tick();
    }
    if (phaseTime >= COUNTDOWN) {
      phase = 'running';
      phaseTime = 0;
      camLerp = 6;
      countdownText('GO!');
      sfx.go();
    }
  } else if (phase === 'running') {
    const wasTurbo = isTurbo(sim);
    for (const ev of stepSim(sim, course, steer, dt)) {
      if (ev === 'pickup') {
        sfx.coin();
        renderItemSlots(true);
      } else if (ev === 'full') {
        showBagFull();
      } else if (ev === 'coin') {
        sfx.coin();
        bumpCoins();
      } else if (ev === 'turbo') {
        sfx.turbo();
        flash('turbo');
      } else if (ev === 'offroad') {
        sfx.bump();
        flash('offroad');
        shake = Math.max(shake, 0.35);
      } else if (ev === 'finish') {
        sfx.finish();
        phase = 'ending';
        phaseTime = 0;
        postV = sim.v;
      } else if (ev === 'gameover') {
        sfx.over();
        phase = 'ending';
        phaseTime = 0;
        postV = sim.v;
      }
    }
    if (sim.offRoad) shake = Math.max(shake, 0.12 * (sim.v / MAX_V));
    runner.update(dt, sim.v, steer, isTurbo(sim) || wasTurbo);
    placeRunner(sim.dist, sim.lateral, steer * 0.25);
  } else if (phase === 'ending') {
    postV *= Math.exp(-dt * (sim.phase === 'finished' ? 1.6 : 2.5));
    postDist += postV * dt;
    if (postV > 3) runner.update(dt, postV, 0, false);
    else if (sim.phase === 'finished') runner.celebrate(clockTime);
    else runner.idle(clockTime);
    placeRunner(sim.dist + postDist, sim.lateral, 0);
    if (sim.phase === 'finished' && phaseTime > 0.6) camMode = 'finish';
    if (phaseTime > (sim.phase === 'finished' ? 2.4 : 1.6)) {
      if (sim.phase === 'finished') showResult();
      else showGameOver();
    }
  }

  // ghost keeps running on its own clock, offset to the left lane
  const gt = phase === 'running' || phase === 'ending' ? sim.ghostDist : 0;
  const gp = worldAt(course, gt, -1.6);
  ghost.group.position.set(gp.x, 0, gp.z);
  ghost.group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(gp.tx, gp.tz);
  if (gt > 0 && gt < course.length) ghost.update(dt, MAX_V * 0.8, 0, false);
  else ghost.idle(clockTime + 1);
  // A ghost just behind the runner sits between runner and camera: fade it out so it never blocks the view.
  const behind = sim.dist + postDist - gt;
  const ghostAlpha = 0.42 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 1 - (behind - 0.5) / 3.5));
  ghost.group.traverse((o) => {
    if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh) (o.material as THREE.Material).opacity = ghostAlpha;
  });
  ghost.group.visible = ghostAlpha > 0.01;

  updateHud();
}

function realSpeedKmh(v: number): number {
  if (!course) return 0;
  // game units → real metres, game seconds → real seconds (ghost ≙ the real run)
  const mPerUnit = course.realDistanceM / course.length;
  const timeScale = course.realDurationSec / course.ghostDuration;
  return ((v * mPerUnit) / timeScale) * 3.6;
}

function updateHud() {
  if (!course) return;
  $('hud-time').textContent = formatTime(sim.phase === 'finished' ? sim.finishTime : sim.t);
  const gapUnits = sim.dist - sim.ghostDist;
  const gapM = Math.round((Math.abs(gapUnits) / course.length) * course.realDistanceM);
  $('hud-gap-label').textContent =
    phase === 'intro' || phase === 'countdown' || gapM < 3
      ? 'Side by side with past you'
      : gapUnits > 0
        ? `${gapM}m ahead of past you`
        : `${gapM}m behind past you`;
  const ratio = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, gapUnits / GAP_LIMIT));
  const fill = $('hud-gap');
  fill.style.width = `${Math.abs(ratio) * 50}%`;
  fill.style.left = ratio >= 0 ? '50%' : `${50 - Math.abs(ratio) * 50}%`;
  fill.style.background = ratio >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : ratio > -0.6 ? 'var(--orange)' : 'var(--red)';

  const here = sampleAt(course, sim.dist);
  ($('hud-pace').querySelector('.dot') as HTMLElement).style.background = PACE_COLORS[here.bucket];
  $('hud-pace-text').textContent = `${PACE_LABELS[here.bucket]} · real ${formatPace(here.pace)}`;
  $('hud-speed').textContent = realSpeedKmh(phase === 'ending' ? postV : sim.v).toFixed(1);
  // active item effect (sprint > grip > energy)
  const effect = isSprinting(sim)
    ? { label: 'Sprint', left: sim.sprintUntil - sim.t, total: MUSCAT_TIME, cls: 'sprint' }
    : hasGrip(sim)
      ? { label: 'Grip', left: sim.gripUntil - sim.t, total: BANANA_TIME, cls: 'grip' }
      : isEnergized(sim)
        ? { label: 'Energy boost', left: sim.energyUntil - sim.t, total: ENERGY_TIME, cls: 'energy' }
        : null;
  const energyEl = $('hud-energy');
  energyEl.classList.toggle('on', !!effect && sim.phase === 'running');
  if (effect) {
    energyEl.dataset.effect = effect.cls;
    $('hud-energy-label').textContent = effect.label;
    $('hud-energy-bar').style.width = `${(Math.max(0, effect.left) / effect.total) * 100}%`;
  }
  $('hud-coins').textContent = String(sim.coins);
  $('hud-coins-total').textContent = `/ ${course.coins.length}`;
  $('hud-progress').style.width = `${(sim.dist / course.length) * 100}%`;
  const p = worldAt(course, sim.dist, sim.lateral);
  const g = worldAt(course, sim.ghostDist, 0);
  hudMap.draw(p, g);
}

function showResult() {
  if (!course || !selectedRun) return;
  const t = sim.finishTime;
  const prev = getBest(selectedRun.id);
  const prevCoins = getBestCoins(selectedRun.id);
  if (sim.coins > prevCoins) setBestCoins(selectedRun.id, sim.coins);
  const isBest = prev === null || t < prev;
  if (isBest) setBest(selectedRun.id, t);
  $('rs-eyebrow').textContent = isBest && prev !== null ? 'NEW RECORD' : 'FINISH';
  $('rs-title').textContent = isBest && prev !== null ? 'New record!' : 'Finished!';
  $('rs-time').textContent = formatTime(t);
  const diff = course.ghostDuration - t;
  const equivalent = course.realDurationSec * (t / course.ghostDuration);
  $('rs-vs').textContent = `${Math.abs(diff).toFixed(2)}s ${diff >= 0 ? 'faster than past you!' : 'slower than past you'}`;
  const stats: [string, string][] = [
    ['Equiv. time', `${fmtClock(equivalent)}`],
    ['Equiv. pace', formatPace(course.avgPace * (t / course.ghostDuration))],
    ['Coins', `${sim.coins} / ${course.coins.length}${sim.coins > prevCoins && prevCoins > 0 ? ' ★' : ''}`],
    ['Turbo · Items', `${sim.turbos} · ${sim.itemsUsed}`],
    ['Off road', `${sim.offRoadTime.toFixed(1)}s`],
    ['Best', formatTime(isBest ? t : prev!)],
  ];
  const dl = $('rs-stats');
  dl.innerHTML = '';
  for (const [k, v] of stats) {
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.innerHTML = '<dt></dt><dd></dd>';
    d.querySelector('dt')!.textContent = k;
    d.querySelector('dd')!.textContent = v;
    dl.appendChild(d);
  }
  show('result');
}

function showGameOver() {
  if (!course) return;
  const pct = Math.round((sim.dist / course.length) * 100);
  $('ov-text').textContent = `Caught at ${pct}% of the course. Hit the blue turbo pads and stay off the grass to win time back!`;
  show('over');
}

// ---------- camera ----------
function cameraTargets(dt: number) {
  if (!course) return;
  const b = course.bounds;
  if (camMode === 'portrait') {
    // sway around the runner's front so the face stays in view
    const a = runner.group.rotation.y + Math.sin(clockTime * 0.25) * 0.9;
    const r = runner.group.position;
    // narrow screens put UI at the bottom: pull back and aim low so the character sits up top
    const narrow = camera.aspect < 1;
    const dist = narrow ? 9 : 6.5;
    // character select on wide screens: pan right (camera + target) so the runner stands left of the panel
    const pan = camera.aspect > 1.2 ? (screen === 'character' ? 2.3 : screen === 'home' ? -1.8 : 0) : 0;
    const px = Math.cos(a) * pan;
    const pz = -Math.sin(a) * pan;
    tmpPos.set(r.x + Math.sin(a) * dist + px, narrow ? 2.2 : 2.6, r.z + Math.cos(a) * dist + pz);
    tmpLook.set(r.x + px, narrow ? -0.9 : 1.1, r.z + pz);
    fogTo(40, 220, dt);
  } else if (camMode === 'overview') {
    const cx = (b.minX + b.maxX) / 2;
    const cz = (b.minZ + b.maxZ) / 2;
    const ext = Math.max(b.maxX - b.minX, b.maxZ - b.minZ);
    const a = clockTime * 0.05;
    // shift look target so the course sits left of the panel on wide screens
    const aspectShift = camera.aspect > 1.2 ? ext * 0.18 : 0;
    tmpLook.set(cx + Math.cos(a) * aspectShift, 0, cz - Math.sin(a) * aspectShift);
    tmpPos.set(cx + Math.sin(a) * ext * 0.42, ext * 0.55, cz + Math.cos(a) * ext * 0.42);
    const d = tmpPos.distanceTo(tmpLook);
    fogTo(d * 0.9, d * 2.2, dt);
  } else if (camMode === 'chase') {
    // portrait screens are narrow: follow the runner's lateral position more tightly
    const portrait = camera.aspect < 1;
    const d = sim.dist + postDist;
    // Sit behind the runner along a smoothed heading, not at the path point 8.5 units back:
    // on a hairpin that point is on the other leg of the route, right next to the runner.
    const here = worldAt(course, d, sim.lateral * (portrait ? 0.95 : 0.7));
    const a = worldAt(course, d - 4, 0);
    const b = worldAt(course, d + 4, 0);
    let hx = b.x - a.x;
    let hz = b.z - a.z;
    const hl = Math.hypot(hx, hz) || 1;
    hx /= hl;
    hz /= hl;
    // ease the heading so the camera swings smoothly round tight turns
    const k = Math.min(1, dt * 4);
    camHeading.x += (hx - camHeading.x) * k;
    camHeading.y += (hz - camHeading.y) * k;
    camHeading.normalize();
    tmpPos.set(here.x - camHeading.x * 8.5, 4.2, here.z - camHeading.y * 8.5);
    const ahead = worldAt(course, d, sim.lateral * (portrait ? 0.85 : 0.4));
    tmpLook.set(ahead.x + camHeading.x * 10, 1.0, ahead.z + camHeading.y * 10);
    fogTo(70, 320, dt);
  } else {
    const r = runner.group.position;
    const a = clockTime * 0.6;
    tmpPos.set(r.x + Math.sin(a) * 6, 2.4, r.z + Math.cos(a) * 6);
    tmpLook.set(r.x, 1.2, r.z);
  }
}

function fogTo(near: number, far: number, dt: number) {
  const k = Math.min(1, dt * 3);
  fog.near += (near - fog.near) * k;
  fog.far += (far - fog.far) * k;
}

function updateCamera(dt: number) {
  cameraTargets(dt);
  const k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * (camMode === 'overview' ? 2 : camLerp));
  camPos.lerp(tmpPos, k);
  camLook.lerp(tmpLook, k);
  camera.position.copy(camPos);
  if (shake > 0.001) {
    camera.position.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake;
    camera.position.y += (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake;
    shake *= Math.exp(-dt * 8);
  }
  camera.lookAt(camLook);
  const turbo = screen === 'play' && isTurbo(sim);
  const speedFov = screen === 'play' ? (sim.v / MAX_V) * 8 + (isEnergized(sim) ? 4 : 0) : 0;
  const fov = 58 + speedFov + (turbo ? 10 : 0);
  camera.fov += (fov - camera.fov) * Math.min(1, dt * 4);
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
}

// ---------- loop ----------
let lastFrame = performance.now();
function frame(now = performance.now()) {
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastFrame) / 1000);
  lastFrame = now;
  clockTime += dt;
  if (screen === 'play') updatePlay(dt);
  else if (screen === 'result') runner.celebrate(clockTime);
  else runner.idle(clockTime);
  // runner's distance along the course; overview cameras see the whole course
  const racing = screen === 'play' || screen === 'result' || screen === 'over';
  const playerS = camMode === 'overview' ? undefined : racing ? sim.dist + postDist : 0;
  road?.update(clockTime, sim.usedPads, playerS);
  gelsView?.update(clockTime, sim.gelsTaken, playerS);
  fruitsView?.update(clockTime, sim.itemsTaken, playerS);
  coinsView?.update(clockTime, sim.collected, playerS);
  nature?.update(clockTime);
  updateCamera(dt);
  const r = runner.group.position;
  followSun(world.sun, r.x, r.z);
  world.renderer.render(scene, camera);
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  if (screen === 'play') hudMap.layout();
  if (screen === 'preview') previewMap.layout();
});

// Boot: show the first sample course behind the home screen.
function boot() {
  loadCourse(courseFor(SAMPLE_RUNS[0]));
  selectedRun = SAMPLE_RUNS[0];
  camPos.set(runner.group.position.x + 6, 2.6, runner.group.position.z + 6);
  show('home');
  frame();
}
// Gel labels are drawn on a canvas, so wait (briefly) for the display font first.
Promise.race([Promise.all([document.fonts.load('700 48px Lexend'), document.fonts.load('800 48px Lexend'), document.fonts.load('48px "Hanalei Fill"')]), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500))]).finally(boot);

// Dev-only handle for automated play-testing.
if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
  Object.assign(window, {
    __game: {
      get sim() {
        return sim;
      },
      get course() {
        return course;
      },
      get screen() {
        return screen;
      },
    },
  });
}
```

```ts
import { CatmullRomCurve3, Vector3 } from 'three';
import { COIN_SPACING, GEL_EVERY_M, GEL_FINISH_MARGIN_M, MAX_V, ROAD_HALF, SPEED_CAP, TURBO_PAD_HALF_WIDTH } from '../game/constants';
import { cumulativeDistance, projectLocal, simplifyRdp } from './geo';
import { averagePace, bucketOf, fillPaces, paceRange, pointPaces } from './pace';
import { mulberry32 } from './samples';
import type { PaceBucket, Run } from './types';

export interface CourseSample {
  x: number;
  z: number;
  /** unit tangent */
  tx: number;
  tz: number;
  /** signed curvature (1/units); positive = right turn */
  k: number;
  pace: number;
  bucket: PaceBucket;
}

export interface TurboPad {
  /** distance along the course */
  s: number;
  lateral: number;
}

/** A collectible coin, positioned like a turbo pad. */
export type Coin = TurboPad;

export type FruitKind = 'banana' | 'muscat';

/** Banana / shine-muscat pickup lying on the road. */
export interface FruitItem {
  s: number;
  lateral: number;
  kind: FruitKind;
}

/** Energy gel pickup; `km` is the real-run distance it marks. */
export interface Gel {
  s: number;
  lateral: number;
  km: number;
}

export interface Course {
  run: Run;
  /** game units */
  length: number;
  /** evenly spaced by arc length; spacing = length / (samples.length - 1) */
  samples: CourseSample[];
  realDistanceM: number;
  realDurationSec: number;
  avgPace: number;
  paceMin: number;
  paceMax: number;
  /** ghost's game-time (seconds) when reaching each sample, normalized to ghostDuration */
  ghostTime: Float32Array;
  ghostDuration: number;
  turboPads: TurboPad[];
  coins: Coin[];
  gels: Gel[];
  items: FruitItem[];
  bounds: { minX: number; maxX: number; minZ: number; maxZ: number };
}

const N = 1200;

/** 3 km → 1600 units, 5 km → 2400, capped at 3200. Keeps a game around 1–2 minutes. */
export function gameLengthFor(km: number): number {
  return Math.max(1400, Math.min(3200, km * 400 + 400));
}

function hashString(s: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = Math.imul(h ^ s.charCodeAt(i), 16777619);
  return h >>> 0;
}

export function buildCourse(run: Run): Course {
  const cum = cumulativeDistance(run.points);
  const realDistanceM = cum[cum.length - 1];
  if (realDistanceM < 200) throw new Error('Route is too short (needs at least 200 m).');

  // Pace per GPS point; gaps are filled with the user's average pace.
  const avgPace = averagePace(run.points, cum, run.avgPaceSec);
  const paces = fillPaces(pointPaces(run.points, cum), avgPace);
  const { min: paceMin, max: paceMax } = paceRange(paces);

  // Shape: project → simplify → centripetal Catmull-Rom → even arc-length samples.
  const local = projectLocal(run.points);
  const kept = simplifyRdp(local, 3);
  const curve = new CatmullRomCurve3(
    kept.map((i) => new Vector3(local[i].x, 0, -local[i].y)), // north = -z
    false,
    'centripetal',
  );
  const pts = curve.getSpacedPoints(N - 1);
  const rawLen = curve.getLength();
  const length = gameLengthFor(realDistanceM / 1000);
  const scale = length / rawLen;
  const ds = length / (N - 1);

  const samples: CourseSample[] = [];
  let pj = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const p = pts[i];
    const a = pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)];
    const b = pts[Math.min(N - 1, i + 1)];
    const tl = Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.z - a.z) || 1;
    // Map back to real distance by fraction of the route.
    const realD = (i / (N - 1)) * realDistanceM;
    while (pj < cum.length - 2 && cum[pj + 1] < realD) pj++;
    const f = (realD - cum[pj]) / Math.max(1e-6, cum[pj + 1] - cum[pj]);
    const pace = paces[pj] + (paces[pj + 1] - paces[pj]) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, f));
    samples.push({
      x: p.x * scale,
      z: p.z * scale,
      tx: (b.x - a.x) / tl,
      tz: (b.z - a.z) / tl,
      k: 0,
      pace,
      bucket: bucketOf(pace, paceMin, paceMax),
    });
  }

  // Majority filter (±8 samples ≈ ±15 units) so pace hovering on a bucket boundary
  // doesn't paint thin flickering stripes.
  const rawBuckets = samples.map((s) => s.bucket);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const votes = [0, 0, 0, 0];
    for (let j = Math.max(0, i - 8); j <= Math.min(N - 1, i + 8); j++) votes[rawBuckets[j]]++;
    samples[i].bucket = votes.indexOf(Math.max(...votes)) as PaceBucket;
  }

  // Out-and-back stretches and U-turns put two passes of the run on (almost) the same street.
  // Push the later pass sideways so the two roads sit side by side like a two-lane road.
  separateParallelPasses(samples, ds);
  // GPS U-turns can reverse direction within a sample or two; round them so the road
  // (and its inner edge) never folds over itself.
  roundTightTurns(samples, ds);

  // Signed curvature from tangent turn rate, smoothed so tiny GPS wiggles don't jerk the runner.
  const rawK = samples.map((s, i) => {
    const n = samples[Math.min(N - 1, i + 1)];
    const cross = s.tx * n.tz - s.tz * n.tx;
    return cross / ds;
  });
  const W = 5;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    let sum = 0;
    let c = 0;
    for (let j = Math.max(0, i - W); j <= Math.min(N - 1, i + W); j++) {
      sum += rawK[j];
      c++;
    }
    samples[i].k = sum / c;
  }

  // Ghost placeholder: real pacing profile scaled to an analytic time.
  // prepareCourse() replaces it with a simulated run.
  let idealTime = 0;
  let paceTime = 0;
  const paceCum = new Float32Array(N);
  for (let i = 1; i < N; i++) {
    idealTime += ds / (MAX_V * SPEED_CAP[samples[i].bucket]);
    paceTime += samples[i].pace;
    paceCum[i] = paceTime;
  }
  const ghostDuration = idealTime;
  const ghostTime = new Float32Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) ghostTime[i] = (paceCum[i] / paceTime) * ghostDuration;

  // Turbo pads: mostly on fast (green/yellow) stretches, deterministic per run.
  const rnd = mulberry32(hashString(run.id));
  const turboPads: TurboPad[] = [];
  const margin = ROAD_HALF - TURBO_PAD_HALF_WIDTH - 0.3;
  for (let s = 120; s < length - 120; s += 70 + rnd() * 60) {
    const b = samples[Math.round(s / ds)].bucket;
    if (b >= 2 && rnd() < 0.75) continue;
    turboPads.push({ s, lateral: (rnd() * 2 - 1) * margin });
    s += 90;
  }

  // Coins: short rows (straight, diagonal, arc) that reward steering. Own RNG stream
  // so adding coins never moves the turbo pads.
  const crnd = mulberry32(hashString(run.id) ^ 0x9e3779b9);
  const coins: Coin[] = [];
  const cm = ROAD_HALF - 1.1;
  for (let s = 60; s < length - 50; ) {
    const count = 5 + Math.floor(crnd() * 4);
    const kind = crnd();
    const a = (crnd() * 2 - 1) * cm;
    const side = crnd() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
      const u = k / (count - 1);
      const lateral =
        kind < 0.4 ? a : kind < 0.75 ? side * cm * (1 - 2 * u) : side * cm * (Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 1.6 - 0.8);
      const cs = s + k * COIN_SPACING;
      // keep pads readable: no coin sitting on top of one
      if (turboPads.some((p) => Math.abs(p.s - cs) < 4 && Math.abs(p.lateral - lateral) < 2.4)) continue;
      coins.push({ s: cs, lateral: Math.max(-cm, Math.min(cm, lateral)) });
    }
    s += count * COIN_SPACING + 35 + crnd() * 55;
  }

  // Energy gels at every GEL_EVERY_M of the real run (like a race aid station).
  const gels: Gel[] = [];
  for (let m = GEL_EVERY_M; m < realDistanceM - GEL_FINISH_MARGIN_M; m += GEL_EVERY_M) {
    const s = (m / realDistanceM) * length;
    gels.push({ s, lateral: 0, km: m / 1000 });
  }
  // clear coins right around a gel so the pouch stands alone
  for (let i = coins.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (gels.some((g) => Math.abs(g.s - coins[i].s) < 15)) coins.splice(i, 1);
  }

  // Fruit items: one every 260–420 units, own RNG stream so pads/coins/gels never move.
  const irnd = mulberry32(hashString(run.id) ^ 0x51ed27);
  const items: FruitItem[] = [];
  const im = ROAD_HALF - 1.2;
  for (let s = 200; s < length - 150; s += 260 + irnd() * 160) {
    if (turboPads.some((p) => Math.abs(p.s - s) < 6) || gels.some((g) => Math.abs(g.s - s) < 20)) continue;
    const item: FruitItem = { s, lateral: (irnd() * 2 - 1) * im, kind: irnd() < 0.5 ? 'banana' : 'muscat' };
    items.push(item);
    for (let i = coins.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (Math.abs(coins[i].s - s) < 6 && Math.abs(coins[i].lateral - item.lateral) < 1.6) coins.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }

  let minX = Infinity;
  let maxX = -Infinity;
  let minZ = Infinity;
  let maxZ = -Infinity;
  for (const s of samples) {
    minX = Math.min(minX, s.x);
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, s.x);
    minZ = Math.min(minZ, s.z);
    maxZ = Math.max(maxZ, s.z);
  }

  return {
    run,
    length,
    samples,
    realDistanceM,
    // averagePace extrapolates over untimed edges, so this covers the whole route.
    realDurationSec: (avgPace * realDistanceM) / 1000,
    avgPace,
    paceMin,
    paceMax,
    ghostTime,
    ghostDuration,
    turboPads,
    coins,
    gels,
    items,
    bounds: { minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ },
  };
}

/** Centre-to-centre distance between two side-by-side passes: two road widths plus a strip of grass. */
export const LANE_SEPARATION = ROAD_HALF * 2 + 1.5;

/**
 * Where a later stretch of the course runs along an earlier one (parallel or antiparallel,
 * within a road width), shift the later stretch sideways so the roads don't overlap.
 * Perpendicular crossings are left alone. Offsets are smoothed so lanes merge and split
 * gradually, then tangents are recomputed. Mutates `samples`.
 */
export function separateParallelPasses(samples: CourseSample[], ds: number): void {
  const N = samples.length;
  const CELL = LANE_SEPARATION;
  // ignore the last ~25 units: those are just the road you're on bending (U-turn apex)
  const RECENT = Math.ceil(25 / ds);
  const grid = new Map<string, number[]>();
  const px = samples.map((p) => p.x);
  const pz = samples.map((p) => p.z);
  const add = (i: number) => {
    const k = `${Math.floor(px[i] / CELL)},${Math.floor(pz[i] / CELL)}`;
    const list = grid.get(k);
    if (list) list.push(i);
    else grid.set(k, [i]);
  };

  // Pass 1: for each sample, the closest earlier parallel/antiparallel sample and which side of it we're on.
  const bestJ = new Int32Array(N).fill(-1);
  const side = new Float64Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const t = samples[i];
    let bestD = LANE_SEPARATION;
    const cx = Math.floor(px[i] / CELL);
    const cz = Math.floor(pz[i] / CELL);
    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
      for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++)
        for (const j of grid.get(`${cx + dx},${cz + dz}`) ?? []) {
          if (j > i - RECENT) continue;
          const d = Math.hypot(px[i] - px[j], pz[i] - pz[j]);
          // only parallel / antiparallel stretches, not crossings
          if (d < bestD && Math.abs(t.tx * samples[j].tx + t.tz * samples[j].tz) > 0.6) {
            bestD = d;
            bestJ[i] = j;
          }
        }
    if (bestJ[i] >= 0) {
      const o = samples[bestJ[i]];
      side[i] = (px[i] - px[bestJ[i]]) * -o.tz + (pz[i] - pz[bestJ[i]]) * o.tx;
    }
    add(i);
  }

  // Pass 2: group into overlapping stretches (small gaps bridged). Each stretch moves to the
  // side it *exits* toward, so the route never has to cross back over the earlier road.
  const ox = new Float64Array(N);
  const oz = new Float64Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; ) {
    if (bestJ[i] < 0) {
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    let end = i;
    let gap = 0;
    for (let k = i + 1; k < N && gap <= 4; k++) {
      if (bestJ[k] >= 0) {
        end = k;
        gap = 0;
      } else gap++;
    }
    let exit = 0;
    const tail = Math.max(i, end - Math.max(3, Math.floor((end - i) / 4)));
    for (let k = tail; k <= end; k++) if (bestJ[k] >= 0) exit += side[k];
    const sign = exit < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    for (let k = i; k <= end; k++) {
      if (bestJ[k] < 0) continue;
      const o = samples[bestJ[k]];
      // only ever push apart; a pass that is already far enough stays where it is
      const push = sign * side[k] < LANE_SEPARATION ? sign * LANE_SEPARATION - side[k] : 0;
      ox[k] = -o.tz * push;
      oz[k] = o.tx * push;
    }
    i = end + 1;
  }

  // Widen each offset over ±W samples (keep the largest), then average over ±W: lanes ease
  // in and out *outside* the overlapping stretch instead of shrinking at its ends.
  const W = 12;
  const wx = new Float64Array(N);
  const wz = new Float64Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    let m = 0;
    for (let j = Math.max(0, i - W); j <= Math.min(N - 1, i + W); j++) {
      const mag = ox[j] * ox[j] + oz[j] * oz[j];
      if (mag > m) {
        m = mag;
        wx[i] = ox[j];
        wz[i] = oz[j];
      }
    }
  }
  // two box-filter passes = a triangle filter: smooth ramps, no kinks
  const box = (src: Float64Array) => {
    const out = new Float64Array(N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      let sum = 0;
      let c = 0;
      for (let j = Math.max(0, i - W); j <= Math.min(N - 1, i + W); j++) {
        sum += src[j];
        c++;
      }
      out[i] = sum / c;
    }
    return out;
  };
  const fx = box(box(wx));
  const fz = box(box(wz));
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    samples[i].x = px[i] + fx[i];
    samples[i].z = pz[i] + fz[i];
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const a = samples[Math.max(0, i - 1)];
    const b = samples[Math.min(N - 1, i + 1)];
    const tl = Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.z - a.z) || 1;
    samples[i].tx = (b.x - a.x) / tl;
    samples[i].tz = (b.z - a.z) / tl;
  }
}

/** Tightest turn the road can take without its inner edge folding over itself. */
export const MIN_TURN_RADIUS = ROAD_HALF;

/**
 * Rebuild the course line through every `step`-th sample (≈ one lane-gap apart) with a
 * centripetal Catmull-Rom curve and resample it evenly. This rounds GPS cusps — a U-turn
 * that reverses within one sample, or the 1–2 m hop at a turnaround — into curves the road
 * can follow, without looping. Pace data stays attached by sample index. Mutates `samples`.
 */
export function roundTightTurns(samples: CourseSample[], ds: number): void {
  const N = samples.length;
  if (minTurnRadius(samples) >= MIN_TURN_RADIUS) return;
  const orig = samples.map((p) => ({ x: p.x, z: p.z }));
  // retry with a coarser control polygon while any turn is still tighter than the road can bend
  for (const mult of [1, 1.5, 2, 3]) {
    const step = Math.max(2, Math.round((LANE_SEPARATION * mult) / ds));
    let poly: { x: number; z: number }[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < N - 1; i += step) poly.push(orig[i]);
    poly.push(orig[N - 1]);
    // Drop spikes (a control point the path runs straight past and comes back to): corner
    // cutting can't round a spike because there's no area to cut into.
    for (let changed = true; changed && poly.length > 3; ) {
      changed = false;
      for (let i = 1; i < poly.length - 1; i++) {
        const a = poly[i - 1];
        const b = poly[i];
        const c = poly[i + 1];
        const ux = b.x - a.x;
        const uz = b.z - a.z;
        const vx = c.x - b.x;
        const vz = c.z - b.z;
        const cos = (ux * vx + uz * vz) / ((Math.hypot(ux, uz) || 1) * (Math.hypot(vx, vz) || 1));
        if (cos < -0.5) {
          poly.splice(i, 1);
          changed = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    // Chaikin corner cutting (keeps the end points): converges to a quadratic B-spline,
    // which — unlike an interpolating spline — rounds every corner instead of passing through it.
    for (let it = 0; it < 4; it++) {
      const next = [poly[0]];
      for (let i = 0; i < poly.length - 1; i++) {
        const a = poly[i];
        const b = poly[i + 1];
        next.push({ x: a.x * 0.75 + b.x * 0.25, z: a.z * 0.75 + b.z * 0.25 });
        next.push({ x: a.x * 0.25 + b.x * 0.75, z: a.z * 0.25 + b.z * 0.75 });
      }
      next.push(poly[poly.length - 1]);
      poly = next;
    }
    resamplePolyline(poly, samples);
    if (minTurnRadius(samples) >= MIN_TURN_RADIUS) break;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const a = samples[Math.max(0, i - 1)];
    const b = samples[Math.min(N - 1, i + 1)];
    const tl = Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.z - a.z) || 1;
    samples[i].tx = (b.x - a.x) / tl;
    samples[i].tz = (b.z - a.z) / tl;
  }
}

/** Write `out.length` points evenly spaced by arc length along `poly` into `out` (x, z). */
function resamplePolyline(poly: { x: number; z: number }[], out: { x: number; z: number }[]): void {
  const cum = [0];
  for (let i = 1; i < poly.length; i++) cum.push(cum[i - 1] + Math.hypot(poly[i].x - poly[i - 1].x, poly[i].z - poly[i - 1].z));
  const total = cum[cum.length - 1];
  let j = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) {
    const d = (i / (out.length - 1)) * total;
    while (j < poly.length - 2 && cum[j + 1] < d) j++;
    const f = (d - cum[j]) / Math.max(1e-9, cum[j + 1] - cum[j]);
    out[i].x = poly[j].x + (poly[j + 1].x - poly[j].x) * f;
    out[i].z = poly[j].z + (poly[j + 1].z - poly[j].z) * f;
  }
}

/** Smallest local turning radius along the course (chord length ÷ turning angle). */
export function minTurnRadius(samples: { x: number; z: number }[]): number {
  let min = Infinity;
  for (let i = 1; i < samples.length - 1; i++) {
    const ux = samples[i].x - samples[i - 1].x;
    const uz = samples[i].z - samples[i - 1].z;
    const vx = samples[i + 1].x - samples[i].x;
    const vz = samples[i + 1].z - samples[i].z;
    const lu = Math.hypot(ux, uz);
    const lv = Math.hypot(vx, vz);
    if (!lu || !lv) continue;
    const angle = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (ux * vx + uz * vz) / (lu * lv))));
    if (angle > 1e-6) min = Math.min(min, (lu + lv) / 2 / angle);
  }
  return min;
}

/** Interpolated sample at distance s. */
export function sampleAt(course: Course, s: number): CourseSample & { i: number } {
  const n = course.samples.length;
  const ds = course.length / (n - 1);
  const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(n - 1.0001, s / ds));
  const i = Math.floor(f);
  const t = f - i;
  const a = course.samples[i];
  const b = course.samples[i + 1];
  const tx = a.tx + (b.tx - a.tx) * t;
  const tz = a.tz + (b.tz - a.tz) * t;
  const tl = Math.hypot(tx, tz) || 1;
  return {
    i,
    x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t,
    z: a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t,
    tx: tx / tl,
    tz: tz / tl,
    k: a.k + (b.k - a.k) * t,
    pace: a.pace + (b.pace - a.pace) * t,
    bucket: t < 0.5 ? a.bucket : b.bucket,
  };
}

/** World position for (distance along, lateral offset). Right of travel = +lateral. */
export function worldAt(course: Course, s: number, lateral: number): { x: number; z: number; tx: number; tz: number } {
  // beyond either end, extend straight along the end tangent
  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(course.length, s));
  const p = sampleAt(course, clamped);
  const extra = s - clamped;
  // right = forward × up = (-tz, tx)
  return {
    x: p.x + p.tx * extra - p.tz * lateral,
    z: p.z + p.tz * extra + p.tx * lateral,
    tx: p.tx,
    tz: p.tz,
  };
}

/** Ghost distance at game time t (seconds since GO). */
export function ghostDistanceAt(course: Course, t: number): number {
  const gt = course.ghostTime;
  if (t <= 0) return 0;
  if (t >= gt[gt.length - 1]) return course.length;
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = gt.length - 1;
  while (hi - lo > 1) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    if (gt[mid] <= t) lo = mid;
    else hi = mid;
  }
  const f = (t - gt[lo]) / Math.max(1e-6, gt[hi] - gt[lo]);
  const ds = course.length / (gt.length - 1);
  return (lo + f) * ds;
}
```

2204 bytes · sha256 `b5aa3932977eec36dbdf59098f033a2c19b21ee79d81a131bf7cdb94da72b9df`

```ts
import type { GpsPoint, Vec2 } from './types';

const EARTH_R = 6371000;
const RAD = Math.PI / 180;

export function haversine(a: GpsPoint, b: GpsPoint): number {
  const dLat = (b.lat - a.lat) * RAD;
  const dLon = (b.lon - a.lon) * RAD;
  const h =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(a.lat * RAD) * Math.cos(b.lat * RAD) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * EARTH_R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(h));
}

/** Cumulative distance in meters at each point. */
export function cumulativeDistance(points: GpsPoint[]): number[] {
  const out = [0];
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) out.push(out[i - 1] + haversine(points[i - 1], points[i]));
  return out;
}

/** Equirectangular projection around the first point. x = east, y = north, in meters. */
export function projectLocal(points: GpsPoint[]): Vec2[] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];
  const { lat: lat0, lon: lon0 } = points[0];
  const k = Math.cos(lat0 * RAD) * EARTH_R * RAD;
  return points.map((p) => ({ x: (p.lon - lon0) * k, y: (p.lat - lat0) * EARTH_R * RAD }));
}

function perpDistance(p: Vec2, a: Vec2, b: Vec2): number {
  const dx = b.x - a.x;
  const dy = b.y - a.y;
  const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
  if (len2 === 0) return Math.hypot(p.x - a.x, p.y - a.y);
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((p.x - a.x) * dx + (p.y - a.y) * dy) / len2));
  return Math.hypot(p.x - (a.x + t * dx), p.y - (a.y + t * dy));
}

/** Ramer–Douglas–Peucker. Returns the indices of kept points (always includes both ends). */
export function simplifyRdp(pts: Vec2[], epsilon: number): number[] {
  if (pts.length <= 2) return pts.map((_, i) => i);
  const keep = new Uint8Array(pts.length);
  keep[0] = keep[pts.length - 1] = 1;
  const stack: [number, number][] = [[0, pts.length - 1]];
  while (stack.length) {
    const [s, e] = stack.pop()!;
    let maxD = 0;
    let idx = -1;
    for (let i = s + 1; i < e; i++) {
      const d = perpDistance(pts[i], pts[s], pts[e]);
      if (d > maxD) {
        maxD = d;
        idx = i;
      }
    }
    if (idx >= 0 && maxD > epsilon) {
      keep[idx] = 1;
      stack.push([s, idx], [idx, e]);
    }
  }
  const out: number[] = [];
  keep.forEach((k, i) => k && out.push(i));
  return out;
}
```

### `src/route/gpx.ts`

1368 bytes · sha256 `ffe595d5db7e5a2218bac3eea5194296b724d5c42f1ef74e6be78d626c1312f7`

```ts
import type { GpsPoint, Run } from './types';

const TRKPT = /<(?:\w+:)?trkpt\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/(?:\w+:)?trkpt>|<(?:\w+:)?trkpt\b([^>]*)\/>/g;
const ATTR = (name: string) => new RegExp(`\\b${name}\\s*=\\s*["']([^"']+)["']`);
const TIME = /<(?:\w+:)?time>([^<]+)<\/(?:\w+:)?time>/;
const NAME = /<(?:\w+:)?name>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?([^<\]]+)/;

/** Minimal GPX track parser — regex based so it also runs outside the browser. */
export function parseGpx(xml: string, fallbackName = 'Imported run'): Run {
  const points: GpsPoint[] = [];
  for (const m of xml.matchAll(TRKPT)) {
    const attrs = m[1] ?? m[3] ?? '';
    const lat = Number(attrs.match(ATTR('lat'))?.[1]);
    const lon = Number(attrs.match(ATTR('lon'))?.[1]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
    const timeStr = m[2]?.match(TIME)?.[1];
    const ms = timeStr ? Date.parse(timeStr.trim()) : NaN;
    points.push(Number.isFinite(ms) ? { lat, lon, t: ms / 1000 } : { lat, lon });
  }
  if (points.length < 2) throw new Error('The GPX file needs at least 2 track points.');
  const firstT = points.find((p) => p.t !== undefined)?.t;
  return {
    id: `gpx-${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
    name: xml.match(NAME)?.[1]?.trim() || fallbackName,
    date: firstT ? new Date(firstT * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    points,
  };
}
```

### `src/route/pace.ts`

2956 bytes · sha256 `db37c303678d742c4a699b666dd37c7cf13ab483d0ea3bdf3e9f40b54f228256`

```ts
import type { GpsPoint, PaceBucket } from './types';

/** Half-window (meters) used to smooth pace around each point. */
const WINDOW_M = 40;
const MIN_PACE = 150; // 2:30 /km — anything faster is GPS noise
const MAX_PACE = 1200; // 20:00 /km — standing still

/**
 * Pace (sec/km) at each point, smoothed over ±WINDOW_M.
 * Returns null where the window lacks timestamps.
 */
export function pointPaces(points: GpsPoint[], cumDist: number[]): (number | null)[] {
  const n = points.length;
  const out: (number | null)[] = new Array(n).fill(null);
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    while (lo < i && cumDist[i] - cumDist[lo + 1] >= WINDOW_M) lo++;
    if (hi < i) hi = i;
    while (hi < n - 1 && cumDist[hi] - cumDist[i] < WINDOW_M) hi++;
    const a = points[lo].t;
    const b = points[hi].t;
    const dist = cumDist[hi] - cumDist[lo];
    // non-monotonic timestamps are GPS/clock glitches — treat as missing
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined || dist < 5 || b <= a) continue;
    let gap = false;
    for (let j = lo; j <= hi; j++) {
      if (points[j].t === undefined) {
        gap = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (gap) continue;
    const pace = (b - a) / (dist / 1000);
    out[i] = Math.max(MIN_PACE, Math.min(MAX_PACE, pace));
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Average pace of the whole run. Prefers real timestamps from the first and last
 * timed points; falls back to the run's declared average, then 6:00/km.
 */
export function averagePace(points: GpsPoint[], cumDist: number[], declared?: number): number {
  let first = -1;
  let last = -1;
  points.forEach((p, i) => {
    if (p.t === undefined) return;
    if (first < 0) first = i;
    last = i;
  });
  if (first >= 0 && last > first) {
    const dist = cumDist[last] - cumDist[first];
    if (dist > 50) return ((points[last].t! - points[first].t!) / dist) * 1000;
  }
  return declared ?? 360;
}

/** Missing paces are replaced by the user's average pace. */
export function fillPaces(paces: (number | null)[], avg: number): number[] {
  return paces.map((p) => p ?? avg);
}

export function paceRange(paces: number[]): { min: number; max: number } {
  let min = Infinity;
  let max = -Infinity;
  for (const p of paces) {
    if (p < min) min = p;
    if (p > max) max = p;
  }
  return { min, max };
}

/** Split [min, max] into four equal ranges: green, yellow, orange, red. */
export function bucketOf(pace: number, min: number, max: number): PaceBucket {
  if (max - min < 1e-6) return 1;
  const t = (pace - min) / (max - min);
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Math.floor(t * 4))) as PaceBucket;
}

export function formatPace(secPerKm: number): string {
  const s = Math.round(secPerKm);
  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}'${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}"`;
}

export function formatTime(sec: number): string {
  const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
  const s = sec - m * 60;
  return `${m}:${s.toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`;
}
```

8306 bytes · sha256 `d79483e2335b4c2f2204e14153fdc2373ced2dbdc4dd9df1fe51cfbd4312ada5`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import overlapGpx from '../game/fixtures/overlap.gpx?raw';
import { buildCourse, ghostDistanceAt, MIN_TURN_RADIUS, minTurnRadius, sampleAt, worldAt } from './course';
import { ROAD_HALF } from '../game/constants';
import { cumulativeDistance, haversine, projectLocal, simplifyRdp } from './geo';
import { parseGpx } from './gpx';
import { averagePace, bucketOf, fillPaces, pointPaces } from './pace';
import { SAMPLE_RUNS } from './samples';

describe('geo', () => {
  it('haversine ≈ 111 km per degree of latitude', () => {
    expect(haversine({ lat: 37, lon: 127 }, { lat: 38, lon: 127 })).toBeCloseTo(111195, -2);
  });

  it('projectLocal keeps distances and puts north on +y', () => {
    const pts = [
      { lat: 37.5, lon: 127 },
      { lat: 37.501, lon: 127.001 },
    ];
    const [a, b] = projectLocal(pts);
    expect(a).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    expect(b.y).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(b.x).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(Math.hypot(b.x, b.y)).toBeCloseTo(haversine(pts[0], pts[1]), 0);
  });

  it('RDP drops collinear points and keeps corners', () => {
    const pts = [
      { x: 0, y: 0 },
      { x: 5, y: 0.1 },
      { x: 10, y: 0 },
      { x: 10, y: 10 },
    ];
    expect(simplifyRdp(pts, 1)).toEqual([0, 2, 3]);
  });
});

describe('pace', () => {
  it('ignores windows whose timestamps go backwards', () => {
    const pts = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({ lat: 37 + (i * 10) / 111195, lon: 127, t: i < 15 ? i * 3 : i * 3 - 100 }));
    const paces = pointPaces(pts, cumulativeDistance(pts));
    expect(paces[2]).toBeCloseTo(300, -1);
    expect(paces[15]).toBeNull();
  });

  it('splits [min, max] into four equal buckets, fastest = green', () => {
    expect(bucketOf(240, 240, 400)).toBe(0);
    expect(bucketOf(279, 240, 400)).toBe(0);
    expect(bucketOf(281, 240, 400)).toBe(1);
    expect(bucketOf(321, 240, 400)).toBe(2);
    expect(bucketOf(400, 240, 400)).toBe(3);
  });

  it('computes pace from timestamps and fills gaps with the average', () => {
    // 10 m per point, 3 s per point → 300 s/km
    const pts = Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => ({
      lat: 37 + (i * 10) / 111195,
      lon: 127,
      t: i >= 20 && i < 30 ? undefined : i * 3,
    }));
    const cum = cumulativeDistance(pts);
    const paces = pointPaces(pts, cum);
    expect(paces[5]).toBeCloseTo(300, -1);
    expect(paces[25]).toBeNull();
    const avg = averagePace(pts, cum);
    expect(avg).toBeCloseTo(300, -1);
    expect(fillPaces(paces, avg)[25]).toBe(avg);
  });
});

describe('gpx', () => {
  it('parses track points, time and name', () => {
    const run = parseGpx(`<?xml version="1.0"?><gpx><trk><name>Morning Run</name><trkseg>
      <trkpt lat="37.5" lon="127.0"><ele>10</ele><time>2026-10-01T00:00:00Z</time></trkpt>
      <trkpt lat="37.501" lon="127.0"><time>2026-10-01T00:00:30Z</time></trkpt>
      <trkpt lat="37.502" lon="127.0"/>
    </trkseg></trk></gpx>`);
    expect(run.name).toBe('Morning Run');
    expect(run.points).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(run.points[1].t! - run.points[0].t!).toBe(30);
    expect(run.points[2].t).toBeUndefined();
    expect(run.date).toBe('2026-10-01');
  });

  it('rejects files without a track', () => {
    expect(() => parseGpx('<gpx></gpx>')).toThrow();
  });
});

describe('course', () => {
  for (const run of SAMPLE_RUNS) {
    it(`${run.name}: builds a playable course`, () => {
      const c = buildCourse(run);
      expect(c.realDistanceM).toBeGreaterThan(2500);
      expect(c.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1400);
      // all four pace colours should appear in a varied run
      const buckets = new Set(c.samples.map((s) => s.bucket));
      expect(buckets.size).toBe(4);
      // ghost time is monotonic and ends at ghostDuration
      for (let i = 1; i < c.ghostTime.length; i++) expect(c.ghostTime[i]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(c.ghostTime[i - 1]);
      expect(c.ghostTime[c.ghostTime.length - 1]).toBeCloseTo(c.ghostDuration, 3);
      expect(c.ghostDuration).toBeGreaterThan(50);
      expect(c.ghostDuration).toBeLessThan(150);
      expect(c.turboPads.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
      // coins: plenty, on the road, sorted along the course, never on a pad
      expect(c.coins.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
      for (let i = 0; i < c.coins.length; i++) {
        const coin = c.coins[i];
        expect(Math.abs(coin.lateral)).toBeLessThan(4.5);
        expect(coin.s).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(coin.s).toBeLessThan(c.length);
        if (i) expect(coin.s).toBeGreaterThan(c.coins[i - 1].s);
        expect(c.turboPads.some((p) => Math.abs(p.s - coin.s) < 4 && Math.abs(p.lateral - coin.lateral) < 2.4)).toBe(false);
      }
      // tangents are unit length
      for (const s of c.samples) expect(Math.hypot(s.tx, s.tz)).toBeCloseTo(1, 3);
    });
  }

  it('places an energy gel at every 5 km of the real run, not at the finish', () => {
    const byId = (id: string) => buildCourse(SAMPLE_RUNS.find((r) => r.id === id)!);
    const ten = byId('yeouido-10k');
    expect(ten.realDistanceM).toBeGreaterThan(9500);
    expect(ten.gels.map((g) => g.km)).toEqual([5]);
    expect(ten.gels[0].s / ten.length).toBeCloseTo(5000 / ten.realDistanceM, 5);
    // no coin crowds the pouch
    expect(ten.coins.some((c) => Math.abs(c.s - ten.gels[0].s) < 15)).toBe(false);
    // a 5.0 km run ends right at the 5 km mark → no gel on the finish line
    expect(byId('riverside-5k').gels).toEqual([]);
    expect(byId('olympic-intervals-3k').gels).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('separates an out-and-back route into side-by-side lanes but keeps crossings', () => {
    const c = buildCourse(parseGpx(overlapGpx));
    const S = c.samples;
    const ds = c.length / (S.length - 1);
    // compare stretches at least ~120 units apart along the course (a U-turn's tip is allowed to pinch)
    const recent = Math.ceil(120 / ds);
    const minGap = ROAD_HALF * 2 * 0.9;
    // longest continuous run of samples lying on top of an earlier, parallel stretch of road
    let run = 0;
    let longest = 0;
    let crossings = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < S.length; i++) {
      let overlapped = false;
      for (let j = 0; j < i - recent; j++) {
        const d = Math.hypot(S[i].x - S[j].x, S[i].z - S[j].z);
        const dot = Math.abs(S[i].tx * S[j].tx + S[i].tz * S[j].tz);
        if (dot > 0.85 && d < minGap) overlapped = true;
        if (dot < 0.3 && d < 3) crossings++;
      }
      run = overlapped ? run + 1 : 0;
      longest = Math.max(longest, run);
    }
    // the 900 m return leg used to sit on the outgoing street for ~340 samples; now only
    // a brief shallow crossing remains (where the route crosses over to the loops)
    expect(longest * ds).toBeLessThan(60);
    // the figure-8 still crosses itself
    expect(crossings).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    // tangents stay unit length, and the path never folds back on itself between neighbouring
    // samples (a GPS U-turn that reversed within one sample used to do exactly that).
    // Note: the very tip of an extreme cusp may still pinch below MIN_TURN_RADIUS.
    for (let i = 0; i < S.length; i++) {
      expect(Math.hypot(S[i].tx, S[i].tz)).toBeCloseTo(1, 3);
      if (i) expect(S[i].tx * S[i - 1].tx + S[i].tz * S[i - 1].tz).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it('real sample routes have no turn tighter than the road can bend', () => {
    for (const run of SAMPLE_RUNS) expect(minTurnRadius(buildCourse(run).samples)).toBeGreaterThan(MIN_TURN_RADIUS);
  });

  it('ghostDistanceAt inverts ghostTime', () => {
    const c = buildCourse(SAMPLE_RUNS[0]);
    expect(ghostDistanceAt(c, 0)).toBe(0);
    expect(ghostDistanceAt(c, c.ghostDuration + 1)).toBe(c.length);
    const mid = ghostDistanceAt(c, c.ghostDuration / 2);
    expect(mid).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(mid).toBeLessThan(c.length);
  });

  it('worldAt offsets to the right of travel', () => {
    const c = buildCourse(SAMPLE_RUNS[0]);
    const p = sampleAt(c, 500);
    const r = worldAt(c, 500, 2);
    // (r - p) · forward ≈ 0 and its cross with forward is positive (right side)
    const dx = r.x - p.x;
    const dz = r.z - p.z;
    expect(dx * p.tx + dz * p.tz).toBeCloseTo(0, 5);
    expect(Math.hypot(dx, dz)).toBeCloseTo(2, 5);
    expect(p.tx * dz - p.tz * dx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});
```

### `src/route/samples.ts`

4528 bytes · sha256 `4f133d69566a2b6d18b6eec297ef362a868fe0eee7bf17074e50f90eac078228`

```ts
import type { GpsPoint, Run } from './types';

/** Deterministic PRNG so sample routes are identical on every load. */
export function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

interface SynthOptions {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  date: string;
  seed: number;
  center: [number, number];
  /** Path in local meters, sampled by parameter u ∈ [0, 1]. */
  shape: (u: number) => [number, number];
  /** Pace (sec/km) at distance d meters along the path. */
  pace: (d: number, total: number) => number;
  /** Distance ranges (meters) where the watch recorded no timestamps. */
  timeGaps?: [number, number][];
}

/** Builds a fake-but-plausible GPS recording: ~8 m spacing, GPS jitter, timestamps from pace. */
function synthesize(o: SynthOptions): Run {
  const rnd = mulberry32(o.seed);
  const dense: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 4000; i++) dense.push(o.shape(i / 4000));
  const cum = [0];
  for (let i = 1; i < dense.length; i++)
    cum.push(cum[i - 1] + Math.hypot(dense[i][0] - dense[i - 1][0], dense[i][1] - dense[i - 1][1]));
  const total = cum[cum.length - 1];

  const [lat0, lon0] = o.center;
  const mPerDegLat = 111320;
  const mPerDegLon = 111320 * Math.cos((lat0 * Math.PI) / 180);
  const points: GpsPoint[] = [];
  let t = Date.parse(`${o.date}T07:00:00+09:00`) / 1000;
  let j = 0;
  let prevD = 0;
  for (let d = 0; d <= total; d += 8) {
    while (j < cum.length - 2 && cum[j + 1] < d) j++;
    const f = (d - cum[j]) / Math.max(1e-6, cum[j + 1] - cum[j]);
    const x = dense[j][0] + (dense[j + 1][0] - dense[j][0]) * f + (rnd() - 0.5) * 3;
    const y = dense[j][1] + (dense[j + 1][1] - dense[j][1]) * f + (rnd() - 0.5) * 3;
    t += ((d - prevD) / 1000) * o.pace(d, total) * (0.97 + rnd() * 0.06);
    prevD = d;
    const inGap = o.timeGaps?.some(([a, b]) => d >= a && d <= b);
    const p: GpsPoint = { lat: lat0 + y / mPerDegLat, lon: lon0 + x / mPerDegLon };
    if (!inGap) p.t = Math.round(t);
    points.push(p);
  }
  return { id: o.id, name: o.name, date: o.date, points };
}

const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

export const SAMPLE_RUNS: Run[] = [
  synthesize({
    id: 'yeouido-10k',
    name: 'Yeouido Long Run 10K',
    date: '2026-10-12',
    seed: 53,
    center: [37.5271, 126.9326],
    shape: (u) => {
      const a = u * TAU * 0.93;
      const r = 1 + 0.08 * Math.sin(a * 4 + 1.1) + 0.04 * Math.sin(a * 9);
      return [Math.cos(a) * 2050 * r, Math.sin(a) * 950 * r];
    },
    // steady start, mid-race dip, tired last 2 km, kick at the end
    pace: (d, total) => {
      const u = d / total;
      return 325 + 15 * Math.sin(u * TAU * 3) + (u > 0.45 && u < 0.55 ? 45 : 0) + (u > 0.75 ? 40 * (u - 0.75) * 4 : 0) - (u > 0.96 ? 70 : 0);
    },
  }),
  synthesize({
    id: 'riverside-5k',
    name: 'Han River Side 5K',
    date: '2026-10-11',
    seed: 11,
    center: [37.5112, 126.9965],
    shape: (u) => {
      const a = u * TAU * 0.92;
      const r = 1 + 0.12 * Math.sin(a * 3 + 0.4) + 0.05 * Math.sin(a * 7);
      return [Math.cos(a) * 1060 * r, Math.sin(a) * 450 * r];
    },
    pace: (d, total) => {
      const u = d / total;
      return 310 - 25 * Math.sin(u * TAU * 2) + (u > 0.85 ? -35 : 0) + (u > 0.42 && u < 0.5 ? 70 : 0);
    },
  }),
  synthesize({
    id: 'namsan-hills-4k',
    name: 'Namsan Hills 4K',
    date: '2026-10-08',
    seed: 23,
    center: [37.5512, 126.9882],
    shape: (u) => {
      const a = u * TAU * 0.85;
      const r = 530 + 110 * Math.sin(a * 5);
      const zig = 75 * Math.sin(u * TAU * 9);
      return [Math.cos(a) * r + zig, Math.sin(a) * r * 0.8];
    },
    pace: (d, total) => {
      const u = d / total;
      const hill = Math.max(0, Math.sin(u * TAU * 3 - 0.6));
      return 345 + 140 * hill * hill - 20 * Math.cos(u * TAU * 7);
    },
  }),
  synthesize({
    id: 'olympic-intervals-3k',
    name: 'Olympic Park Intervals 3K',
    date: '2026-10-03',
    seed: 37,
    center: [37.5206, 127.1214],
    shape: (u) => {
      const a = u * TAU * 0.9;
      return [
        Math.cos(a) * 540 + 100 * Math.sin(a * 4),
        Math.sin(a) * 420 + 70 * Math.cos(a * 3),
      ];
    },
    // 400 m fast / 200 m easy
    pace: (d) => (d % 600 < 400 ? 255 : 400),
    // Watch lost time sync for a stretch — filled with the average pace.
    timeGaps: [[1900, 2350]],
  }),
];
```
