### `src/route/types.ts`

531 bytes · sha256 `553bf5c6d8ab45666bd3c0773a9f24b3106040e23d39943b4d5485f5e6858c68`

```ts
/** One GPS fix. `t` is unix seconds; missing when the device didn't record time. */
export interface GpsPoint {
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  t?: number;
}

export interface Run {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** ISO date of the real run */
  date: string;
  points: GpsPoint[];
  /** Fallback average pace (sec/km) when timestamps are missing entirely. */
  avgPaceSec?: number;
}

export interface Vec2 {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

/** 0 = green (fastest) … 3 = red (slowest) */
export type PaceBucket = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
```

### `src/style.css`

20721 bytes · sha256 `8057706d2fc1629999d408a59ae831386cc7f76c01bc3b97a8c7093dfd967b24`

```css
:root {
  --ink: #2b2233;
  --ink-soft: #6b6275;
  --paper: #fffaf2;
  --paper-2: #fff3e3;
  --coral: #ff8a7a;
  --coral-dark: #f06a5a;
  --sky: #8ecbf0;
  --green: #3fc39a;
  --yellow: #f5d04f;
  --orange: #f5a04f;
  --red: #ee6767;
  --shadow: 0 10px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.12), 0 20px 40px rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.12);
  --font: 'Lexend', system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-title: 'Hanalei Fill', 'Lexend', system-ui, sans-serif;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  margin: 0;
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #ffe6cc;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-family: var(--font);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  user-select: none;
  touch-action: none;
}

#gl {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}

h1,
h2 {
  margin: 0;
  /* Hanalei Fill ships one weight — never fake-bold it */
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-weight: 400;
  font-synthesis: none;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

/* ---------- screens ---------- */
.screen,
.hud {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  display: none;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.screen.active,
.hud.active {
  display: flex;
}
.screen > * {
  pointer-events: auto;
}

#screen-home {
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-start;
  padding: 0 7vw;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.85) 0%, rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.4) 45%, transparent 70%);
}
.home {
  /* the home text fills the left ~60% of the screen; the character stands on the right */
  max-width: min(52vw, 760px);
}
.logo {
  /* sized to the viewport: "RUNNER" spans ~3.3em, two lines ~1.85em tall */
  font-size: min(13vw, 22vh);
  line-height: 0.92;
  color: var(--coral);
  margin: 0 0 0.12em;
}
.home .lead {
  font-size: clamp(18px, min(1.9vw, 3vh), 26px);
  margin-bottom: 1.1em;
}
.home .btn.big {
  font-size: clamp(18px, min(1.6vw, 2.6vh), 24px);
  padding: 0.85em 1.6em;
  width: min(100%, 480px);
}

.lead {
  font-size: 18px;
  line-height: 1.55;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  margin: 0 0 28px;
}
.eyebrow {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.hint {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  line-height: 1.5;
  min-height: 1.5em;
}

#screen-select,
#screen-preview,
#screen-character,
#screen-result,
#screen-over {
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 16px;
  background: rgba(255, 240, 225, 0.25);
}
#screen-preview,
#screen-character {
  justify-content: flex-end;
  padding-right: 5vw;
  background: none;
}

.panel {
  position: relative;
  background: var(--paper);
  border-radius: 28px;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  padding: 28px;
  width: min(440px, 100%);
  max-height: calc(100% - 16px);
  overflow: auto;
}
.panel h2 {
  font-size: 34px;
  margin: 4px 0 12px;
}
.row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
}
.row.actions {
  justify-content: stretch;
  margin-top: 20px;
}
.row.actions .btn {
  flex: 1;
}

/* ---------- buttons ---------- */
.btn {
  font-family: var(--font);
  font-size: 17px;
  font-weight: 600;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 14px 22px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: transform 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.12s ease;
  color: var(--ink);
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 5px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.15);
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}
.btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}
.btn:active {
  transform: translateY(3px);
  box-shadow: 0 2px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.15);
}
.btn.primary {
  background: var(--coral);
  color: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 5px 0 var(--coral-dark);
}
.btn.big {
  font-size: 20px;
  padding: 18px 30px;
  width: 100%;
}
.btn.small {
  font-size: 14px;
  padding: 9px 16px;
}
.btn.ghost {
  background: var(--paper-2);
}
.btn:disabled {
  opacity: 0.6;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.back {
  position: absolute;
  top: 18px;
  right: 18px;
  border: 0;
  background: var(--paper-2);
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  font-size: 18px;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: var(--ink);
}

/* ---------- character picker ---------- */
.pick-label {
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}
.char-picker {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 22px;
}
.char-option {
  font-family: var(--font);
  border: 3px solid transparent;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  border-radius: 20px;
  padding: 6px 6px 8px;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 2px;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.1);
  transition: transform 0.12s ease, border-color 0.12s ease;
}
.char-option:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}
.char-option.selected {
  border-color: var(--coral);
  background: #fff;
}
.char-option img {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 96px;
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #d9effa, #fff3e3);
}
.char-option small {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  text-align: center;
  line-height: 1.3;
}
.panel.character {
  width: min(660px, 100%);
}
.character .char-picker {
  margin: 16px 0 22px;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 8px;
}
.character .char-option {
  font-size: 16px;
  padding: 6px 4px 8px;
}
.character .char-option img {
  max-width: 120px;
}

/* ---------- routes grid (rewards) ---------- */
#screen-select {
  padding: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(232, 222, 248, 0.72), rgba(255, 236, 226, 0.78));
  backdrop-filter: blur(14px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(14px);
}
.routes {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: max(28px, env(safe-area-inset-top)) clamp(16px, 4vw, 48px) 48px;
}
.routes-head {
  position: relative;
  max-width: 1120px;
  margin: 0 auto 26px;
  text-align: center;
}
.routes-head .back {
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: auto;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
}
.routes-title {
  font-size: clamp(48px, 8vw, 84px);
  margin: 6px 0 6px;
}
.routes-sum {
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-weight: 500;
}
.route-grid {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 0;
  max-width: 1120px;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, 250px), 1fr));
  gap: 18px;
}
.route-card {
  --tint-a: #fde4ec;
  --tint-b: #e6efff;
  background: #fffaf4;
  border-radius: 26px;
  overflow: hidden;
  cursor: pointer;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.07), 0 18px 34px rgba(142, 114, 184, 0.14);
  transition: transform 0.18s ease, box-shadow 0.18s ease;
  outline: none;
}
.route-card:hover,
.route-card:focus-visible {
  transform: translateY(-4px) rotate(-0.4deg);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.08), 0 26px 44px rgba(142, 114, 184, 0.22);
}
.route-card.tint-1 {
  --tint-a: #e3f0ff;
  --tint-b: #fff4d6;
}
.route-card.tint-2 {
  --tint-a: #fff2cf;
  --tint-b: #fde3ec;
}
.route-card.tint-3 {
  --tint-a: #def6ee;
  --tint-b: #ece6ff;
}
.route-art {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 4 / 3;
  background: radial-gradient(120% 90% at 20% 10%, #ffffff 0%, transparent 55%), linear-gradient(150deg, var(--tint-a), var(--tint-b));
}
.route-art canvas {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}
.route-km {
  position: absolute;
  right: 12px;
  top: 12px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 4px 11px;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
}
.route-info {
  padding: 14px 16px 16px;
}
.route-info h3 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.2;
}
.route-date {
  margin: 3px 0 10px;
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.route-stats {
  display: flex;
  gap: 14px;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.route-stats .t::before {
  content: '⏱ ';
}
.route-stats .p::before {
  content: '↗ ';
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.route-badges {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 12px;
}
.badge {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  background: var(--paper-2);
}
.badge.best {
  background: #ffe9a8;
  color: #8a5a00;
}
.badge.new {
  background: #e6dcff;
  color: #5b3fa0;
}
.badge.coin::before {
  content: '$ ';
  color: #e39a2d;
}
.badge.gel {
  background: #ffdbe0;
  color: #b8283a;
}
.route-card.add {
  background: transparent;
  box-shadow: none;
  border: 3px dashed rgba(142, 114, 184, 0.45);
  display: grid;
  min-height: 200px;
}
.route-card.add label {
  display: grid;
  place-content: center;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: #6b5a8e;
}
.route-card.add .plus {
  width: 54px;
  height: 54px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font-size: 32px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}
.route-card.add small {
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

/* ---------- my stats ---------- */
#screen-stats {
  padding: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(232, 222, 248, 0.72), rgba(255, 236, 226, 0.78));
  backdrop-filter: blur(14px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(14px);
}
.home .btn.ghost.big {
  margin-top: 12px;
}
.routes-stats {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
}
.stats-grid {
  max-width: 1120px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 440px), 1fr));
  gap: 18px;
}
.stat-card {
  background: #fffaf4;
  border-radius: 26px;
  padding: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.07), 0 18px 34px rgba(142, 114, 184, 0.14);
}
.stat-card.wide,
.stat-card.main-card {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
}
.stat-card h3 {
  margin: 0 0 4px;
  font-size: 18px;
}
.stat-card .hint {
  margin: 0 0 12px;
  min-height: 0;
}
.main-card .char-picker {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));
  margin: 0;
}
.char-option.is-main::after {
  content: 'Main';
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #fff;
  background: var(--coral);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 1px 8px;
  margin-top: 2px;
}
.stats.summary {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  margin: 0;
}
.stats.summary small {
  display: block;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  margin-top: 2px;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
.pace-bar {
  display: flex;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}
.pace-legend b {
  margin-left: auto;
}
.pace-legend li {
  justify-content: flex-start;
}
.bests {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
}
.bests li {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 36px 1fr auto auto;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  background: #fff;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 10px 14px;
}
.bests .medal {
  font-size: 22px;
  text-align: center;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.bests .name {
  font-weight: 600;
}
.bests .name small {
  display: block;
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.bests .time {
  font-weight: 700;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.bests .coins {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: var(--paper-2);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
}

/* ---------- preview ---------- */
.map-big {
  width: 100%;
  aspect-ratio: 1.35;
  background: #eef7e3;
  border-radius: 20px;
  display: block;
  margin: 6px 0 14px;
}
.stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 8px;
  margin: 0 0 14px;
}
.stats div {
  background: var(--paper-2);
  border-radius: 14px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
}
.stats dt {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.stats dd {
  margin: 2px 0 0;
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.legend {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 0 0 6px;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 6px 12px;
  font-size: 13px;
}
.legend li {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}
.legend i {
  width: 22px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  flex: none;
}
.legend small {
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
.hud {
  display: none;
  padding: max(16px, env(safe-area-inset-top)) 16px 16px;
}
.hud.active {
  display: block;
}
.chip {
  white-space: nowrap;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.92);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.1);
}
.hud-tl {
  position: absolute;
  top: 16px;
  left: 16px;
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
}
.hud-row {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  align-items: center;
}
.chip.coins {
  font-size: 22px;
  padding: 8px 14px 8px 10px;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.chip.coins small {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-weight: 500;
}
.coin-icon {
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #fdbd4e;
  border: 3px solid #ffd668;
  box-shadow: 2px 1px 0 #f39a3d;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
}
.coin-icon::after {
  content: '$';
  font-style: normal;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #ffe08a;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 0 #e9a23d;
}
.energy {
  display: none;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  background: #e8414f;
  color: #fff;
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 14px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 #b92c38;
  width: max-content;
}
.energy.on {
  display: flex;
}
.energy[data-effect='grip'] {
  background: #e0a81e;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 #b07f10;
}
.energy[data-effect='sprint'] {
  background: #6fae2a;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 #4f8418;
}
.hud-items {
  position: absolute;
  left: 16px;
  bottom: max(20px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  pointer-events: auto;
}
.item-slot {
  position: relative;
  width: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  border-radius: 18px;
  border: 2px dashed rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.18);
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.7);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  padding: 0;
}
.item-slot.full {
  border: 2px solid #fff;
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.95);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.12);
}
.item-slot svg {
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
}
.item-slot kbd {
  position: absolute;
  right: -4px;
  bottom: -6px;
  font-family: var(--font);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: var(--ink);
  color: #fff;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 1px 5px;
}
.item-slot:not(.full) kbd {
  opacity: 0.35;
}
.hud-bag {
  position: absolute;
  left: 16px;
  bottom: calc(max(20px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)) + 76px);
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #b8283a;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.gel-icon {
  width: 12px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 4px 4px 3px 3px;
  background: linear-gradient(#f8a9b4 0 22%, #fff 22% 30%, #ffd4da 30%);
}
.energy-bar {
  width: 70px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.energy-bar div {
  height: 100%;
  background: #fff;
}
.countdown.banner {
  font-size: clamp(34px, 6vw, 60px);
  text-shadow: 0 5px 0 #e8414f, 0 10px 24px rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.25);
  align-items: start;
  padding-top: 22vh;
}
.chip.time {
  font-size: 30px;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  padding: 8px 20px;
}
.gap {
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.92);
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 8px 12px 10px;
  width: 230px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.1);
}
.gap-label {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}
.gap-bar {
  position: relative;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #ece3d6;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.gap-fill {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  left: 50%;
  width: 0;
  background: var(--green);
}
.gap-mid {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  top: -2px;
  bottom: -2px;
  width: 2px;
  background: var(--ink);
}
.hud-tr {
  position: absolute;
  top: 16px;
  right: 16px;
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
  justify-items: end;
}
.map-small {
  width: 190px;
  height: 160px;
  background: rgba(238, 247, 227, 0.88);
  border-radius: 20px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.1);
}
.progress {
  width: 190px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.9);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.progress div {
  height: 100%;
  width: 0;
  background: var(--coral);
}
.hud-quit {
  pointer-events: auto;
  position: absolute;
  top: 196px;
  right: 16px;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 0;
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.9);
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 14px;
}
.hud-bottom {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: max(20px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}
.chip.pace .dot {
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--green);
}
.speed {
  background: rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.92);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 6px 16px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.1);
}
.speed b {
  font-size: 24px;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.speed small {
  margin-left: 4px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}
.countdown {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font-size: clamp(90px, 18vw, 180px);
  font-weight: 400;
  color: #fff;
  text-shadow: 0 8px 0 var(--coral), 0 14px 30px rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.25);
  pointer-events: none;
}
.flash {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  opacity: 0;
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 45%, rgba(95, 211, 255, 0.55) 100%);
}
.flash.gel {
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 40%, rgba(255, 120, 150, 0.55) 100%);
}
.flash.offroad {
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 50%, rgba(238, 103, 103, 0.4) 100%);
}
.touch-hint {
  position: absolute;
  inset: auto 0 90px 0;
  display: none;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 0 28px;
  font-size: 34px;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  text-shadow: 0 3px 0 rgba(43, 34, 51, 0.2);
}
.touch .touch-hint {
  display: flex;
}

/* ---------- result ---------- */
.result {
  text-align: center;
}
.big-time {
  font-size: 64px;
  font-weight: 400;
  color: var(--coral);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  line-height: 1;
  margin: 8px 0 10px;
}
.vs {
  font-size: 17px;
  line-height: 1.5;
  margin: 0 0 16px;
}
.result .stats {
  text-align: left;
}

.toast {
  position: fixed;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: 28px;
  transform: translate(-50%, 20px);
  background: var(--ink);
  color: #fff;
  padding: 12px 20px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 15px;
  opacity: 0;
  transition: opacity 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.toast.show {
  opacity: 1;
  transform: translate(-50%, 0);
}

/* ---------- mobile ---------- */
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .character .char-picker {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
  .routes-head {
    padding-top: 52px;
  }
  .main-card .char-picker {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
  .bests li {
    grid-template-columns: 28px 1fr auto;
  }
  .bests .coins {
    display: none;
  }
  .routes-stats {
    top: 0;
    right: 0;
  }
  .route-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 12px;
  }
  .route-info {
    padding: 10px 12px 12px;
  }
  .route-info h3 {
    font-size: 15px;
  }
  .route-stats {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 2px;
    font-size: 13px;
  }
  .badge {
    font-size: 11px;
    padding: 3px 8px;
  }
  #screen-home {
    align-items: flex-end;
    padding: 16px 16px 40px;
    background: linear-gradient(0deg, rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.92) 0%, rgba(255, 250, 242, 0.5) 55%, transparent 80%);
  }
  .lead {
    font-size: 16px;
  }
  .home {
    max-width: none;
    width: 100%;
  }
  .logo {
    font-size: min(22vw, 16vh);
  }
  .home .lead {
    font-size: clamp(17px, 4.6vw, 21px);
  }
  .home .btn.big {
    font-size: clamp(18px, 5vw, 22px);
    width: 100%;
  }
  #screen-preview,
  #screen-character {
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: flex-end;
    padding: 16px;
  }
  .panel {
    padding: 22px;
  }
  .map-big {
    aspect-ratio: 1.8;
  }
  .chip.time {
    font-size: 22px;
  }
  .item-slot {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .item-slot svg {
    width: 30px;
    height: 30px;
  }
  .hud-bottom {
    left: auto;
    right: 16px;
    transform: none;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-end;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .chip.coins {
    font-size: 18px;
    padding: 6px 10px 6px 8px;
  }
  .coin-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
  }
  .gap {
    width: 170px;
  }
  .map-small,
  .progress {
    width: 120px;
  }
  .map-small {
    height: 100px;
  }
  .hud-quit {
    top: 136px;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { PACE_COLORS } from '../game/constants';
import type { Course } from '../route/course';
import { mulberry32 } from '../route/samples';

export interface MinimapOptions {
  line?: number;
  pad?: number;
  /** extra top padding (CSS px) to keep the route clear of overlays such as tags */
  padTop?: number;
  /** soft coral glow under the route (reward cards, preview) */
  glow?: boolean;
  /** faint procedural street network behind the route; 0 = off, 1 = full */
  streets?: number;
  /** decorative location pin in the top-left corner */
  pin?: boolean;
}

const CORAL = '#ff8a7a';
const CORAL_DEEP = '#f2677d';

function hashString(s: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = Math.imul(h ^ s.charCodeAt(i), 16777619);
  return h >>> 0;
}

/**
 * North-up route map in a "track on a street map" style: a soft street network fading
 * toward the edges, the route as a glowing pace-coloured line, round start/finish dots.
 * Everything static is cached in an offscreen canvas; each frame only blits and draws dots.
 */
export class Minimap {
  private bg = document.createElement('canvas');
  private course: Course | null = null;
  private scale = 1;
  private ox = 0;
  private oy = 0;
  private dpr = 1;

  constructor(private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, private opts: MinimapOptions = {}) {}

  setCourse(course: Course): void {
    this.course = course;
    this.layout();
  }

  /** Re-measure (call after the canvas becomes visible or resizes). */
  layout(): void {
    const c = this.course;
    if (!c) return;
    this.dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(this.canvas.clientWidth * this.dpr));
    const h = Math.max(1, Math.round(this.canvas.clientHeight * this.dpr));
    this.canvas.width = this.bg.width = w;
    this.canvas.height = this.bg.height = h;

    const pad = (this.opts.pad ?? 14) * this.dpr;
    const padTop = (this.opts.padTop ?? this.opts.pad ?? 14) * this.dpr;
    const b = c.bounds;
    const bw = b.maxX - b.minX || 1;
    const bh = b.maxZ - b.minZ || 1;
    this.scale = Math.min((w - pad * 2) / bw, (h - pad - padTop) / bh);
    this.ox = (w - bw * this.scale) / 2 - b.minX * this.scale;
    this.oy = padTop + (h - pad - padTop - bh * this.scale) / 2 - b.minZ * this.scale;

    const g = this.bg.getContext('2d')!;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    if (this.opts.streets) this.drawStreets(g, w, h, hashString(c.run.id), this.opts.streets);

    g.lineCap = g.lineJoin = 'round';
    const line = (this.opts.line ?? 4) * this.dpr;
    const pts = c.samples;
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(pts.length / 600));
    const path = () => {
      g.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += step) {
        const [x, y] = this.project(pts[i].x, pts[i].z);
        if (i === 0) g.moveTo(x, y);
        else g.lineTo(x, y);
      }
      const end = pts[pts.length - 1];
      g.lineTo(...this.project(end.x, end.z));
    };

    // soft glow + white casing: the route reads as a highlighted track on the map
    if (this.opts.glow) {
      g.save();
      g.shadowColor = 'rgba(255, 120, 130, 0.45)';
      g.shadowBlur = 14 * this.dpr;
      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)';
      g.lineWidth = line + 3 * this.dpr;
      path();
      g.stroke();
      g.restore();
    }
    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)';
    g.lineWidth = line + 3 * this.dpr;
    path();
    g.stroke();

    // pace-coloured runs
    g.lineWidth = line;
    let i = 0;
    while (i < pts.length - 1) {
      const bucket = pts[i].bucket;
      g.strokeStyle = PACE_COLORS[bucket];
      g.beginPath();
      g.moveTo(...this.project(pts[i].x, pts[i].z));
      let j = i;
      while (j < pts.length - 1 && pts[j].bucket === bucket) {
        j = Math.min(pts.length - 1, j + step);
        g.lineTo(...this.project(pts[j].x, pts[j].z));
      }
      g.stroke();
      i = j;
    }

    // energy gel stations
    for (const gel of c.gels) {
      const p = c.samples[Math.round((gel.s / c.length) * (pts.length - 1))];
      const [gx, gy] = this.project(p.x, p.z);
      const gw = 7 * this.dpr;
      const gh = 10 * this.dpr;
      g.fillStyle = '#e8414f';
      g.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
      g.lineWidth = 2 * this.dpr;
      g.beginPath();
      g.roundRect(gx - gw / 2, gy - gh / 2, gw, gh, 2.5 * this.dpr);
      g.fill();
      g.stroke();
    }

    // round start / finish dots (finish has a white centre)
    const end = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const dotR = Math.max(4.5, line / this.dpr + 1.5) * this.dpr;
    this.endDot(g, ...this.project(pts[0].x, pts[0].z), dotR, false);
    this.endDot(g, ...this.project(end.x, end.z), dotR, true);

    if (this.opts.pin) this.drawPin(g, pad * 0.9, pad * 0.75, 15 * this.dpr);
    this.draw();
  }

  private project(x: number, z: number): [number, number] {
    return [x * this.scale + this.ox, z * this.scale + this.oy];
  }

  /**
   * Procedural street network in canvas space: two near-perpendicular families of
   * broken, slightly bent streets plus short alleys, blurred and faded toward the edges.
   */
  private drawStreets(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, w: number, h: number, seed: number, strength: number) {
    const rnd = mulberry32(seed);
    const layer = document.createElement('canvas');
    layer.width = w;
    layer.height = h;
    const s = layer.getContext('2d')!;
    const span = Math.min(w, h);
    const spacing = span / (3.4 + rnd() * 1.2);
    const diag = Math.hypot(w, h);
    const cx = w / 2;
    const cy = h / 2;
    const theta = (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.6;
    const families = [theta, theta + Math.PI / 2 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.15];
    const streets: [number, number][][] = [];

    for (const a of families) {
      const dx = Math.cos(a);
      const dy = Math.sin(a);
      const nx = -dy;
      const ny = dx;
      const count = Math.ceil(diag / spacing) + 1;
      for (let k = -count; k <= count; k++) {
        const off = k * spacing + (rnd() - 0.5) * spacing * 0.35;
        // walk along the street, breaking it into pieces with gaps
        let t = -diag / 2;
        while (t < diag / 2) {
          const len = spacing * (0.8 + rnd() * 2.2);
          if (rnd() > 0.22) {
            const piece: [number, number][] = [];
            const n = 3;
            for (let p = 0; p <= n; p++) {
              const tt = t + (len * p) / n;
              const bend = (rnd() - 0.5) * spacing * 0.12;
              piece.push([cx + dx * tt + nx * (off + bend), cy + dy * tt + ny * (off + bend)]);
            }
            streets.push(piece);
          }
          t += len + spacing * (0.15 + rnd() * 0.35);
        }
      }
      // short alleys branching across this family
      for (let k = 0; k < 14; k++) {
        const px = rnd() * w;
        const py = rnd() * h;
        const len = spacing * (0.25 + rnd() * 0.5);
        streets.push([
          [px, py],
          [px + nx * len, py + ny * len],
        ]);
      }
    }

    const stroke = (width: number, color: string, blur: number) => {
      s.filter = blur ? `blur(${blur}px)` : 'none';
      s.strokeStyle = color;
      s.lineWidth = width;
      s.lineCap = s.lineJoin = 'round';
      for (const st of streets) {
        s.beginPath();
        s.moveTo(st[0][0], st[0][1]);
        for (let p = 1; p < st.length; p++) s.lineTo(st[p][0], st[p][1]);
        s.stroke();
      }
    };
    // faint lilac edge so white streets still read on pale backgrounds
    stroke(spacing * 0.24, 'rgba(142,114,184,0.10)', 3 * this.dpr);
    stroke(spacing * 0.2, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)', 2.5 * this.dpr);
    stroke(spacing * 0.075, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)', 0);
    s.filter = 'none';

    // fade toward the edges like a vignette
    s.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
    const fade = s.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, span * 0.2, cx, cy, Math.max(w, h) * 0.62);
    fade.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)');
    fade.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    s.fillStyle = fade;
    s.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

    g.save();
    g.globalAlpha = strength;
    g.drawImage(layer, 0, 0);
    g.restore();
  }

  private endDot(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, r: number, finish: boolean) {
    g.save();
    g.shadowColor = 'rgba(242, 103, 125, 0.45)';
    g.shadowBlur = 8 * this.dpr;
    const grad = g.createLinearGradient(x - r, y - r, x + r, y + r);
    grad.addColorStop(0, CORAL);
    grad.addColorStop(1, CORAL_DEEP);
    g.fillStyle = grad;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
    g.restore();
    g.lineWidth = 2 * this.dpr;
    g.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
    g.stroke();
    if (finish) {
      g.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      g.beginPath();
      g.arc(x, y, r * 0.38, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      g.fill();
    }
  }

  /** Teardrop map pin with a coral→yellow gradient and a white hole. */
  private drawPin(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, size: number) {
    const r = size * 0.5;
    const cx = x + r;
    const cy = y + r;
    g.save();
    g.shadowColor = 'rgba(242, 103, 125, 0.35)';
    g.shadowBlur = 6 * this.dpr;
    const grad = g.createLinearGradient(cx, y, cx, y + size * 1.45);
    grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffc46b');
    grad.addColorStop(1, CORAL_DEEP);
    g.fillStyle = grad;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(cx, cy, r, Math.PI * 0.85, Math.PI * 2.15);
    g.lineTo(cx, y + size * 1.45);
    g.closePath();
    g.fill();
    g.restore();
    g.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(cx, cy, r * 0.38, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
  }

  draw(player?: { x: number; z: number }, ghost?: { x: number; z: number }): void {
    const g = this.canvas.getContext('2d')!;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);
    g.drawImage(this.bg, 0, 0);
    const dot = (p: { x: number; z: number }, r: number, fill: string) => {
      const [x, y] = this.project(p.x, p.z);
      g.beginPath();
      g.arc(x, y, r * this.dpr, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      g.fillStyle = fill;
      g.fill();
      g.lineWidth = 2 * this.dpr;
      g.strokeStyle = '#2b2233';
      g.stroke();
    };
    if (ghost) dot(ghost, 4.5, '#bfe6ff');
    if (player) dot(player, 6, '#ff8a7a');
  }
}
```

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { toon } from './scene';

export type CharacterId = 'hoodie' | 'sprout' | 'dizzy' | 'bloom' | 'beanie';

export const CHARACTERS: { id: CharacterId; name: string; blurb: string }[] = [
  { id: 'hoodie', name: 'Hoodie', blurb: 'Mint hood kid' },
  { id: 'sprout', name: 'Sprout', blurb: 'Leafy smiler' },
  { id: 'dizzy', name: 'Dizzy', blurb: 'Giggly sprout' },
  { id: 'bloom', name: 'Bloom', blurb: 'Walking flower pot' },
  { id: 'beanie', name: 'Beanie', blurb: 'Cozy beanie walker' },
];

export interface Runner {
  group: THREE.Group;
  /** speed in units/s drives the stride; steer leans the body */
  update(dt: number, speed: number, steer: number, turbo: boolean): void;
  /** idle bounce for menus / countdown */
  idle(time: number): void;
  celebrate(time: number): void;
}

/** A colour, or a bottom→top gradient painted into vertex colours. */
type Paint = string | string[];

/** Pivots every character exposes so the shared animation can drive it. */
interface Rig {
  body: THREE.Group;
  head: THREE.Group;
  arms: [THREE.Group, THREE.Group];
  legs: [THREE.Group, THREE.Group];
  /** hip height of the leg pivots */
  hipY: number;
  /** resting outward spread of the arms (radians) */
  armRest: number;
}

interface Kit {
  mesh(geo: THREE.BufferGeometry, paint: Paint): THREE.Mesh;
  ghost: boolean;
}

/** Bake a vertical gradient (stops evenly spaced bottom→top) into vertex colours. */
function paintGradient(geo: THREE.BufferGeometry, stops: string[]): void {
  geo.computeBoundingBox();
  const { min, max } = geo.boundingBox!;
  const pos = geo.getAttribute('position');
  const cols = stops.map((s) => new THREE.Color(s));
  const out = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);
  const c = new THREE.Color();
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const t = (pos.getY(i) - min.y) / Math.max(1e-6, max.y - min.y);
    const f = t * (cols.length - 1);
    const k = Math.min(cols.length - 2, Math.floor(f));
    c.copy(cols[k]).lerp(cols[k + 1], f - k);
    out.set([c.r, c.g, c.b], i * 3);
  }
  geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(out, 3));
}

function makeKit(ghost: boolean): Kit {
  const ghostMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#bfe6ff', transparent: true, opacity: 0.42, depthWrite: false });
  return {
    ghost,
    mesh(geo, paint) {
      if (ghost) return new THREE.Mesh(geo, ghostMat);
      if (Array.isArray(paint)) {
        paintGradient(geo, paint);
        return new THREE.Mesh(geo, toon('#ffffff', { vertexColors: true }));
      }
      return new THREE.Mesh(geo, toon(paint));
    },
  };
}

const sphere = (r: number, w = 28, h = 20) => new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, w, h);
const capsule = (r: number, len: number) => new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(r, len, 8, 16);

/** Limb that hangs from a pivot: capsule of `len` plus an optional tip mesh at the end. */
function limb(kit: Kit, len: number, r: number, paint: Paint, tip?: THREE.Object3D): THREE.Group {
  const pivot = new THREE.Group();
  const m = kit.mesh(capsule(r, len), paint);
  m.position.y = -len / 2 - r * 0.5;
  pivot.add(m);
  if (tip) {
    tip.position.y = -len - r;
    pivot.add(tip);
  }
  return pivot;
}

interface FaceParts {
  eyes: 'googly' | 'happy' | 'beads' | 'x';
  eyeX: number;
  eyeY: number;
  /** depth of the face surface */
  z: number;
  mouth: 'smile' | 'lips' | 'open';
  lipColor?: string;
  cheeks?: boolean;
  nose?: boolean;
}

function face(kit: Kit, head: THREE.Group, parts: FaceParts) {
  if (kit.ghost) return;
  const black = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#2b2233' });
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const ex = sx * parts.eyeX;
    if (parts.eyes === 'googly') {
      const white = new THREE.Mesh(sphere(0.13, 20, 14), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ffffff' }));
      white.scale.set(1, 1, 0.45);
      white.position.set(ex, parts.eyeY, parts.z);
      const pupil = new THREE.Mesh(sphere(0.09, 16, 12), black);
      pupil.scale.set(1, 1, 0.45);
      pupil.position.set(ex, parts.eyeY, parts.z + 0.045);
      head.add(white, pupil);
    } else if (parts.eyes === 'happy') {
      // closed, smiling ∩ eyes
      const arc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.085, 0.022, 8, 16, Math.PI), black);
      arc.position.set(ex, parts.eyeY, parts.z);
      head.add(arc);
    } else if (parts.eyes === 'beads') {
      // glossy black bead eyes with a tiny highlight
      const bead = kit.mesh(sphere(0.105, 20, 14), '#2b2233');
      bead.scale.set(1, 1, 0.6);
      bead.position.set(ex, parts.eyeY, parts.z);
      const glint = new THREE.Mesh(sphere(0.025, 8, 6), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ffffff' }));
      glint.position.set(ex - 0.03, parts.eyeY + 0.035, parts.z + 0.06);
      head.add(bead, glint);
    } else {
      // ✕ eyes: two crossed capsules
      for (const r of [Math.PI / 4, -Math.PI / 4]) {
        const bar = new THREE.Mesh(capsule(0.024, 0.13), black);
        bar.rotation.z = r;
        bar.position.set(ex, parts.eyeY, parts.z);
        head.add(bar);
      }
    }
    if (parts.cheeks) {
      const cheek = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.085, 16),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ff9fb4', transparent: true, opacity: 0.85 }),
      );
      cheek.position.set(sx * (parts.eyeX + 0.12), parts.eyeY - 0.13, parts.z - 0.04);
      cheek.rotation.y = sx * 0.45;
      head.add(cheek);
    }
  }
  if (parts.nose) {
    const nose = kit.mesh(sphere(0.065, 14, 10), '#f07d5e');
    nose.position.set(0, parts.eyeY - 0.12, parts.z + 0.03);
    head.add(nose);
  }
  if (parts.mouth === 'lips') {
    const lips = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.11, 0.055, 10, 24), parts.lipColor ?? '#f2876a');
    lips.scale.set(1.35, 0.55, 0.8);
    lips.position.set(0, parts.eyeY - 0.28, parts.z - 0.02);
    head.add(lips);
  } else if (parts.mouth === 'open') {
    // big laughing mouth: shallow lower-half bowls that bulge out of the curved head
    const y = parts.eyeY - 0.2;
    const bowl = (r: number) => new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, 24, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 2);
    const mouth = new THREE.Mesh(bowl(0.17), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#e5435a' }));
    mouth.scale.set(1, 0.95, 0.5);
    mouth.position.set(0, y, parts.z);
    const tongue = new THREE.Mesh(bowl(0.1), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ff8f9c' }));
    tongue.scale.set(1.1, 0.7, 0.5);
    tongue.position.set(0, y - 0.07, parts.z + 0.045);
    const lip = new THREE.Mesh(capsule(0.016, 0.3), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#b83246' }));
    lip.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
    lip.position.set(0, y, parts.z + 0.05);
    head.add(mouth, tongue, lip);
  } else {
    const mouth = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.06, 0.018, 6, 14, Math.PI), black);
    mouth.position.set(0, parts.eyeY - 0.17, parts.z);
    mouth.rotation.z = Math.PI;
    head.add(mouth);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Reference 1: yellow→mint hoodie, teal bangs, googly eyes, rain boots. */
function buildHoodie(kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group): Rig {
  const skin = '#f6c1b4';
  const hoodY = ['#ffe8a8', '#ffe2a0'];
  const body = new THREE.Group();
  root.add(body);

  const torso = kit.mesh(sphere(0.55), ['#5fcfcb', '#c8ecd0', '#ffe6a4']);
  torso.scale.set(1.0, 0.82, 0.86);
  torso.position.y = 1.0;
  body.add(torso);
  // drawstrings
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const cord = kit.mesh(capsule(0.018, 0.16), '#4fc6c2');
    cord.position.set(sx * 0.09, 1.3, 0.45);
    cord.rotation.z = sx * 0.25;
    const tip = kit.mesh(sphere(0.04, 10, 8), '#4fc6c2');
    tip.position.set(sx * 0.11, 1.2, 0.46);
    body.add(cord, tip);
  }

  const head = new THREE.Group();
  head.position.y = 1.86;
  body.add(head);
  const hood = kit.mesh(sphere(0.66, 32, 24), hoodY);
  hood.scale.set(1, 1, 0.92);
  const rim = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.47, 0.13, 14, 36), '#ffe1a0');
  rim.position.z = 0.42;
  const faceDisc = kit.mesh(sphere(0.48, 28, 20), skin);
  faceDisc.scale.set(1, 1, 0.42);
  faceDisc.position.z = 0.4;
  head.add(hood, rim, faceDisc);
  // teal bangs: two rounded halves meeting at a centre part
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const bang = kit.mesh(sphere(0.3, 20, 14), '#7fd7d2');
    bang.scale.set(0.95, 0.62, 0.42);
    bang.position.set(sx * 0.19, 0.27, 0.5);
    bang.rotation.z = sx * -0.35;
    head.add(bang);
  }
  face(kit, head, { eyes: 'googly', eyeX: 0.15, eyeY: -0.02, z: 0.6, mouth: 'smile', cheeks: true });

  const arms: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const legs: THREE.Group[] = [];
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    // puffy sleeve with a little pink hand poking out
    const hand = kit.mesh(sphere(0.09, 12, 8), skin);
    const arm = limb(kit, 0.26, 0.16, ['#7fd8cf', '#ffe6a4'], hand);
    arm.position.set(sx * 0.5, 1.22, 0);
    body.add(arm);
    arms.push(arm);

    const boot = new THREE.Group();
    const shaft = kit.mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.13, 0.2, 16), ['#8fe0d0', '#ffe6a4']);
    shaft.position.y = 0.08;
    const toe = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 16, 12), '#7fdcd0');
    toe.scale.set(1, 0.62, 1.5);
    toe.position.set(0, -0.04, 0.07);
    boot.add(shaft, toe);
    const leg = limb(kit, 0.36, 0.055, skin, boot);
    leg.position.set(sx * 0.17, 0.72, 0);
    root.add(leg);
    legs.push(leg);
  }
  root.scale.setScalar(0.85);
  return { body, head, arms: arms as Rig['arms'], legs: legs as Rig['legs'], hipY: 0.72, armRest: 0.35 };
}

/** Reference 2: gradient bulb head with sprout leaves, happy eyes, big lips, purple belly. */
function buildSprout(kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group): Rig {
  const mint = '#79dccb';
  const body = new THREE.Group();
  root.add(body);

  const torso = kit.mesh(sphere(0.5), ['#c27be0', '#e6b2df', '#f7dccb']);
  torso.scale.set(1, 1.05, 0.92);
  torso.position.y = 0.98;
  body.add(torso);
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const pad = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 14, 10), '#f1bde8');
    pad.scale.set(0.7, 1, 1);
    pad.position.set(sx * 0.44, 1.2, 0);
    body.add(pad);
  }

  const head = new THREE.Group();
  head.position.y = 1.98;
  body.add(head);
  const bulb = kit.mesh(sphere(0.62, 32, 24), ['#5fcfc4', '#cfe8b4', '#fbecbc']);
  bulb.scale.set(1, 1.05, 0.98);
  head.add(bulb);
  for (const [y, c] of [
    [0.64, '#dca6ef'],
    [0.74, '#f3c4f0'],
  ] as const) {
    const ring = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.17, 0.06, 10, 24), c);
    ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    ring.position.y = y;
    head.add(ring);
  }
  for (const [rz, ry] of [
    [0.95, 0],
    [-0.95, 0],
    [0.15, 0.4],
  ]) {
    const leaf = kit.mesh(capsule(0.085, 0.28), ['#a8d98a', '#f3eeb0']);
    leaf.position.set(Math.sin(-rz) * 0.17, 0.98, 0);
    leaf.rotation.set(0, ry, rz);
    head.add(leaf);
  }
  face(kit, head, { eyes: 'happy', eyeX: 0.21, eyeY: 0.12, z: 0.58, mouth: 'lips', nose: true });

  const arms: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const legs: THREE.Group[] = [];
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const arm = limb(kit, 0.42, 0.11, mint);
    arm.position.set(sx * 0.46, 1.18, 0);
    body.add(arm);
    arms.push(arm);

    const boot = new THREE.Group();
    const cuff = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.075, 0.035, 8, 16), '#fbe39a');
    cuff.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    cuff.position.y = 0.1;
    const toe = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 16, 12), '#e7a6f2');
    toe.scale.set(1, 0.65, 1.5);
    toe.position.set(0, -0.03, 0.07);
    boot.add(cuff, toe);
    const leg = limb(kit, 0.32, 0.065, mint, boot);
    leg.position.set(sx * 0.16, 0.66, 0);
    root.add(leg);
    legs.push(leg);
  }
  root.scale.setScalar(0.82);
  return { body, head, arms: arms as Rig['arms'], legs: legs as Rig['legs'], hipY: 0.66, armRest: 0.55 };
}

/** Reference 3: peach→yellow bulb head with leaves in a teal ring, ✕ eyes, laughing mouth, teal body. */
function buildDizzy(kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group): Rig {
  const pink = '#f4b8b2';
  const teal = '#3fb5b0';
  const body = new THREE.Group();
  root.add(body);

  const torso = kit.mesh(sphere(0.46), teal);
  torso.scale.set(1, 1.02, 0.92);
  torso.position.y = 0.98;
  const shorts = kit.mesh(sphere(0.43), '#5ec7bf');
  shorts.scale.set(1.04, 0.5, 0.95);
  shorts.position.y = 0.7;
  body.add(torso, shorts);
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const cap = kit.mesh(sphere(0.13, 14, 10), teal);
    cap.position.set(sx * 0.42, 1.17, 0);
    body.add(cap);
  }

  const head = new THREE.Group();
  head.position.y = 1.98;
  body.add(head);
  const bulb = kit.mesh(sphere(0.64, 32, 24), ['#f19f68', '#f7c07c', '#fbd98f']);
  head.add(bulb);
  for (const [y, c] of [
    [0.63, '#4cbdb6'],
    [0.72, '#3fb0aa'],
  ] as const) {
    const ring = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.17, 0.06, 10, 24), c);
    ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    ring.position.y = y;
    head.add(ring);
  }
  for (const [rz, ry] of [
    [1.0, 0],
    [-1.0, 0],
    [0.35, 0.6],
    [-0.25, -0.6],
  ]) {
    const leaf = kit.mesh(capsule(0.085, 0.3), ['#f0cd86', '#fbe9b8']);
    leaf.position.set(Math.sin(-rz) * 0.18, 0.98, 0);
    leaf.rotation.set(0, ry, rz);
    head.add(leaf);
  }
  face(kit, head, { eyes: 'x', eyeX: 0.2, eyeY: 0.12, z: 0.6, mouth: 'open' });

  const arms: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const legs: THREE.Group[] = [];
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const arm = limb(kit, 0.42, 0.09, pink);
    arm.position.set(sx * 0.47, 1.16, 0);
    body.add(arm);
    arms.push(arm);

    const boot = new THREE.Group();
    const cuff = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.085, 0.04, 8, 16), '#f6c46a');
    cuff.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    cuff.position.y = 0.1;
    const toe = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 16, 12), '#ee8b68');
    toe.scale.set(1, 0.65, 1.5);
    toe.position.set(0, -0.03, 0.07);
    boot.add(cuff, toe);
    const leg = limb(kit, 0.32, 0.075, pink, boot);
    leg.position.set(sx * 0.17, 0.64, 0);
    root.add(leg);
    legs.push(leg);
  }
  root.scale.setScalar(0.82);
  return { body, head, arms: arms as Rig['arms'], legs: legs as Rig['legs'], hipY: 0.64, armRest: 0.5 };
}

/** Reference 4: the head *is* the body — an open flower pot (mint→lilac→pink) with leaves and a pink bloom. */
function buildBloom(kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group): Rig {
  const pink = '#f3b9df';
  const body = new THREE.Group();
  root.add(body);
  const head = new THREE.Group();
  head.position.y = 1.28;
  body.add(head);

  // pot: a sphere with its top cap removed, rendered double-sided so the inside shows
  const opening = 0.52;
  const pot = kit.mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.74, 36, 26, 0, Math.PI * 2, opening, Math.PI - opening), [
    '#e58fdc',
    '#cdb3e6',
    '#9fd9d6',
    '#7fdcc8',
  ]);
  (pot.material as THREE.Material).side = THREE.DoubleSide;
  pot.scale.set(1, 0.97, 0.95);
  head.add(pot);
  const rimY = 0.74 * Math.cos(opening) * 0.97;
  const rimR = 0.74 * Math.sin(opening);
  const rim = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(rimR, 0.045, 10, 36), '#86e0cc');
  rim.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  rim.position.y = rimY;
  const soil = kit.mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(rimR, 28), '#4aa892');
  soil.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  soil.position.y = rimY - 0.05;
  head.add(rim, soil);
  // a crown of mint leaves leaning outward
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    const pivot = new THREE.Group();
    pivot.position.y = rimY - 0.04;
    pivot.rotation.y = (i / 7) * Math.PI * 2;
    const leaf = kit.mesh(capsule(0.07, 0.2), ['#4fc7a6', '#86e3c6']);
    leaf.scale.set(1.5, 1, 0.55);
    leaf.position.set(0.13, 0.16, 0);
    leaf.rotation.z = -0.45;
    pivot.add(leaf);
    head.add(pivot);
  }
  // curved stem with a pink flower
  const stemCurve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
    new THREE.Vector3(0, rimY - 0.05, 0),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.02, rimY + 0.35, 0),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.1, rimY + 0.62, 0.02),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.26, rimY + 0.8, 0.05),
  ]);
  head.add(kit.mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(stemCurve, 24, 0.025, 8, false), '#4fbf9c'));
  const flower = new THREE.Group();
  flower.position.set(0.3, rimY + 0.86, 0.07);
  flower.rotation.set(-0.25, -0.35, 0.35);
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const a = (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2;
    const petal = kit.mesh(sphere(0.1, 14, 10), '#e78ae8');
    petal.scale.set(1.15, 1.15, 0.55);
    petal.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 0.13, Math.sin(a) * 0.13, 0);
    flower.add(petal);
  }
  const center = kit.mesh(sphere(0.08, 14, 10), '#f6d44a');
  center.scale.set(1, 1, 0.7);
  center.position.z = 0.04;
  flower.add(center);
  head.add(flower);
  face(kit, head, { eyes: 'beads', eyeX: 0.17, eyeY: 0.05, z: 0.66, mouth: 'lips', lipColor: '#f6d23f' });

  const arms: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const legs: THREE.Group[] = [];
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const arm = limb(kit, 0.62, 0.075, pink, kit.mesh(sphere(0.085, 12, 8), pink));
    arm.position.set(sx * 0.72, 1.45, 0);
    body.add(arm);
    arms.push(arm);

    const boot = new THREE.Group();
    const cuff = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.08, 0.03, 8, 16), '#86e0cc');
    cuff.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    cuff.position.y = 0.09;
    const toe = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 16, 12), ['#e58fdc', '#9fdcd6']);
    toe.scale.set(1, 0.65, 1.5);
    toe.position.set(0, -0.03, 0.07);
    boot.add(cuff, toe);
    const leg = limb(kit, 0.34, 0.07, pink, boot);
    leg.position.set(sx * 0.18, 0.6, 0);
    root.add(leg);
    legs.push(leg);
  }
  root.scale.setScalar(0.82);
  return { body, head, arms: arms as Rig['arms'], legs: legs as Rig['legs'], hipY: 0.6, armRest: 0.06 };
}

/** Reference 5: teal head in a rolled pale-yellow beanie, googly eyes, yellow→coral bell body. */
function buildBeanie(kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group): Rig {
  const teal = '#3db4b8';
  const body = new THREE.Group();
  root.add(body);

  // bell-shaped body from a lathe profile (radius, height)
  const profile = [
    [0.001, 0],
    [0.46, 0],
    [0.5, 0.05],
    [0.49, 0.2],
    [0.44, 0.4],
    [0.34, 0.6],
    [0.2, 0.74],
    [0.001, 0.8],
  ].map(([x, y]) => new THREE.Vector2(x, y));
  const bell = kit.mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(profile, 40), ['#f19478', '#f6b07f', '#fbd88e']);
  bell.position.y = 0.5;
  body.add(bell);

  const head = new THREE.Group();
  head.position.y = 1.72;
  body.add(head);
  head.add(kit.mesh(sphere(0.56, 32, 24), teal));
  const dome = kit.mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 32, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), '#f8dfa2');
  dome.scale.set(1.06, 0.95, 1.06);
  dome.position.y = 0.22;
  const brim = kit.mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.52, 0.11, 14, 40), '#fae5b0');
  brim.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  brim.position.y = 0.24;
  head.add(dome, brim);
  face(kit, head, { eyes: 'googly', eyeX: 0.16, eyeY: -0.04, z: 0.52, mouth: 'smile', cheeks: true });

  const arms: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const legs: THREE.Group[] = [];
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    const arm = limb(kit, 0.3, 0.065, teal, kit.mesh(sphere(0.08, 12, 8), teal));
    const sleeve = kit.mesh(capsule(0.11, 0.08), '#f7b48a');
    sleeve.position.y = -0.06;
    arm.add(sleeve);
    arm.position.set(sx * 0.4, 1.1, 0);
    body.add(arm);
    arms.push(arm);

    const boot = new THREE.Group();
    const cuff = kit.mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.085, 0.085, 0.08, 16), '#f8d98e');
    cuff.position.y = 0.09;
    const toe = kit.mesh(sphere(0.15, 16, 12), '#f39f74');
    toe.scale.set(1, 0.65, 1.5);
    toe.position.set(0, -0.03, 0.07);
    boot.add(cuff, toe);
    const leg = limb(kit, 0.36, 0.06, teal, boot);
    leg.position.set(sx * 0.16, 0.6, 0);
    root.add(leg);
    legs.push(leg);
  }
  root.scale.setScalar(0.85);
  return { body, head, arms: arms as Rig['arms'], legs: legs as Rig['legs'], hipY: 0.6, armRest: 0.3 };
}

const BUILDERS: Record<CharacterId, (kit: Kit, root: THREE.Group) => Rig> = {
  hoodie: buildHoodie,
  sprout: buildSprout,
  dizzy: buildDizzy,
  bloom: buildBloom,
  beanie: buildBeanie,
};

/** Cute rounded toy character; all variants share the same rig and animation. */
export function createRunner(opts: { ghost?: boolean; kind?: CharacterId } = {}): Runner {
  const ghost = !!opts.ghost;
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const inner = new THREE.Group(); // per-character scale lives here
  group.add(inner);
  const rig = BUILDERS[opts.kind ?? 'hoodie'](makeKit(ghost), inner);
  const { body, head, arms, legs, hipY, armRest } = rig;

  group.traverse((o) => {
    if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh) o.castShadow = !ghost;
  });

  let phase = 0;
  let lean = 0;

  const pose = (swing: number, bob: number) => {
    legs[0].rotation.x = swing;
    legs[1].rotation.x = -swing;
    arms[0].rotation.set(-swing * 1.1, 0, -armRest);
    arms[1].rotation.set(swing * 1.1, 0, armRest);
    body.position.y = bob;
    legs[0].position.y = legs[1].position.y = hipY + bob * 0.5;
  };

  return {
    group,
    update(dt, speed, steer, turbo) {
      // stride frequency grows with speed but saturates — toy legs can only go so fast
      phase += dt * (4 + Math.min(speed, 45) * 0.32);
      const swing = Math.sin(phase) * Math.min(1.1, 0.3 + speed * 0.03);
      pose(swing, Math.abs(Math.cos(phase)) * 0.12);
      lean += (-steer * 0.25 - lean) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
      body.rotation.z = lean;
      body.rotation.x = turbo ? 0.35 : 0.12 + speed * 0.003;
      head.rotation.set(-body.rotation.x * 0.5, 0, 0);
    },
    idle(time) {
      pose(Math.sin(time * 2) * 0.08, Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 2.4)) * 0.05);
      body.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
      head.rotation.set(0, Math.sin(time * 0.8) * 0.25, 0);
    },
    celebrate(time) {
      const j = Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 6));
      body.position.y = j * 0.5;
      legs[0].position.y = legs[1].position.y = hipY + j * 0.5;
      arms[0].rotation.set(Math.PI, 0, -0.5 - j * 0.3);
      arms[1].rotation.set(Math.PI, 0, 0.5 + j * 0.3);
      body.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    },
  };
}

/** Renders a small portrait of each character (the real 3D model) to a data URL. */
export function renderThumbnails(size = 192): Record<CharacterId, string> {
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, preserveDrawingBuffer: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(1);
  renderer.setSize(size, size, false);
  renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffffff', '#c9b9a8', 2.2));
  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#fff1d6', 1.8);
  sun.position.set(2, 4, 5);
  scene.add(sun);
  const cam = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(30, 1, 0.1, 50);
  cam.position.set(0.5, 1.6, 5.5);
  cam.lookAt(0, 1.28, 0);

  const out = {} as Record<CharacterId, string>;
  for (const { id } of CHARACTERS) {
    const r = createRunner({ kind: id });
    r.idle(0.3);
    r.group.rotation.y = -0.25;
    scene.add(r.group);
    renderer.render(scene, cam);
    out[id] = renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/png');
    scene.remove(r.group);
    r.group.traverse((o) => {
      if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh) {
        o.geometry.dispose();
        (o.material as THREE.Material).dispose();
      }
    });
  }
  renderer.dispose();
  renderer.forceContextLoss();
  return out;
}
```

4251 bytes · sha256 `0e1af1dec38e71da64a047819d31bbfe6783c679fcaa862cada835a32930509a`

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { mergeGeometries } from 'three/examples/jsm/utils/BufferGeometryUtils.js';
import { worldAt, type Course } from '../route/course';
import { pickupVisible } from './road';
import { toon } from './scene';

export interface CoinsView {
  group: THREE.Group;
  /** collected = indices picked up in the current run */
  update(time: number, collected: Set<number>, playerS?: number): void;
}

const POP_TIME = 0.28;
const R = 0.7;
const HALF_T = 0.12;

/** Raised "$": an S-curve tube plus short stubs above and below, facing +z. */
function dollarGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const pts = [
    [0.17, 0.19],
    [0.07, 0.26],
    [-0.08, 0.26],
    [-0.17, 0.17],
    [-0.14, 0.05],
    [0, 0],
    [0.14, -0.05],
    [0.17, -0.17],
    [0.08, -0.26],
    [-0.07, -0.26],
    [-0.17, -0.19],
  ].map(([x, y]) => new THREE.Vector3(x, y, 0));
  const s = new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts), 64, 0.05, 10, false);
  const top = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.045, 0.08, 4, 10).translate(0, 0.35, 0);
  const bottom = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.045, 0.08, 4, 10).translate(0, -0.35, 0);
  return mergeGeometries([s, top, bottom])!;
}

/** Both faces of a part: the front copy at +z, a mirrored copy at -z. */
function bothFaces(geo: THREE.BufferGeometry, z: number): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const front = geo.clone().translate(0, 0, z);
  const back = geo.clone().rotateY(Math.PI).translate(0, 0, -z);
  return mergeGeometries([front, back])!;
}

/**
 * Soft matte toy coin (reference: rounded gold coin with a raised $):
 * orange edge band, slightly orange recessed face, thick pale-gold rim and symbol.
 */
export function coinParts(): { edge: THREE.BufferGeometry; face: THREE.BufferGeometry; gold: THREE.BufferGeometry } {
  const edge = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R, R, HALF_T * 2, 48, 1, true).rotateX(Math.PI / 2);
  const face = bothFaces(new THREE.CircleGeometry(R - 0.08, 48), HALF_T);
  const rim = bothFaces(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R - 0.1, 0.1, 14, 56), HALF_T);
  const dollar = bothFaces(dollarGeometry(), HALF_T + 0.01);
  const gold = mergeGeometries([rim, dollar])!;
  return { edge, face, gold };
}

export const COIN_COLORS = { edge: '#f39a3d', face: '#fdbd4e', gold: '#ffd668' };

/** Spinning, bobbing coins; picked-up coins pop up and vanish. */
export function buildCoins(course: Course): CoinsView {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const n = course.coins.length;
  const parts = coinParts();
  const meshes = (Object.keys(parts) as (keyof typeof parts)[]).map((k) => {
    const m = new THREE.InstancedMesh(parts[k], toon(COIN_COLORS[k], { side: k === 'edge' ? THREE.DoubleSide : THREE.FrontSide }), n);
    m.castShadow = k === 'edge';
    group.add(m);
    return m;
  });

  const base = course.coins.map((c) => {
    const p = worldAt(course, c.s, c.lateral);
    return new THREE.Vector3(p.x, 1.1, p.z);
  });
  const pickedAt = new Float32Array(n).fill(-1);
  let seen = 0;

  const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4();
  const q = new THREE.Quaternion();
  const e = new THREE.Euler();
  const v = new THREE.Vector3();
  const sc = new THREE.Vector3();

  return {
    group,
    update(time, collected, playerS) {
      if (collected.size < seen) pickedAt.fill(-1); // new run
      seen = collected.size;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (collected.has(i) && pickedAt[i] < 0) pickedAt[i] = time;
        const age = pickedAt[i] < 0 ? -1 : time - pickedAt[i];
        let s = pickupVisible(course.coins[i].s, playerS) ? 1 : 0;
        let lift = Math.sin(time * 3 + i * 0.7) * 0.12;
        let spin = time * 2.2 + i * 0.45;
        if (age >= 0) {
          if (age > POP_TIME) {
            s = 0;
          } else {
            const k = age / POP_TIME;
            s = 1 + k * 0.6 - k * k * 1.6;
            lift += k * 2.2;
            spin += k * 12;
          }
        }
        // slight forward tilt like the reference, so the face catches the light
        e.set(-0.18, spin, 0, 'YXZ');
        m4.compose(v.copy(base[i]).setY(base[i].y + lift), q.setFromEuler(e), sc.setScalar(Math.max(0, s)));
        for (const m of meshes) m.setMatrixAt(i, m4);
      }
      for (const m of meshes) m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    },
  };
}
```

### `src/world/fruits.ts`

3686 bytes · sha256 `65c4766658469b5ced1e55482839716521c1e0a11c7ef7eeaa80782b2005dac6`

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { worldAt, type Course } from '../route/course';
import { pickupVisible } from './road';
import { toon } from './scene';

export interface FruitsView {
  group: THREE.Group;
  update(time: number, taken: Set<number>, playerS?: number): void;
}

/** Curved yellow banana with brown tips. */
function banana(): THREE.Group {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
    new THREE.Vector3(-0.55, 0.25, 0),
    new THREE.Vector3(-0.25, -0.08, 0),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.2, -0.12, 0),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.55, 0.22, 0),
  ]);
  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 24, 0.17, 12, false), toon('#ffd84d'));
  g.add(body);
  for (const [t, color] of [
    [0, '#8a5a2b'],
    [1, '#6b8a2b'],
  ] as const) {
    const tip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.1, 10, 8), toon(color));
    tip.position.copy(curve.getPoint(t));
    g.add(tip);
  }
  return g;
}

/** One shine-muscat grape: glossy green sphere with a tiny stem. */
function muscat(): THREE.Group {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const grape = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.42, 28, 20), toon('#b8e86a', { emissive: '#3f6b12', emissiveIntensity: 0.25 }));
  grape.scale.set(1, 1.08, 1);
  const glint = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.09, 12, 8), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#f4ffe0' }));
  glint.position.set(-0.16, 0.2, 0.33);
  const stem = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.035, 0.05, 0.22, 8), toon('#7a9a3a'));
  stem.position.y = 0.52;
  stem.rotation.z = 0.25;
  g.add(grape, glint, stem);
  return g;
}

const POP_TIME = 0.35;

/** Floating banana / shine-muscat pickups with a pulsing ground ring. */
export function buildFruits(course: Course): FruitsView {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const items = course.items.map((item) => {
    const p = worldAt(course, item.s, item.lateral);
    const root = new THREE.Group();
    root.position.set(p.x, 0, p.z);
    const model = item.kind === 'banana' ? banana() : muscat();
    model.scale.setScalar(1.4);
    model.traverse((o) => {
      if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh) o.castShadow = true;
    });
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.RingGeometry(0.9, 1.35, 32),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: item.kind === 'banana' ? '#ffe27a' : '#cdf28a', transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false }),
    );
    ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    ring.position.y = 0.09;
    root.add(model, ring);
    group.add(root);
    return { root, model, ring, pickedAt: -1 };
  });
  let seen = 0;

  return {
    group,
    update(time, taken, playerS) {
      if (taken.size < seen) for (const it of items) it.pickedAt = -1;
      seen = taken.size;
      items.forEach((it, i) => {
        if (taken.has(i) && it.pickedAt < 0) it.pickedAt = time;
        const age = it.pickedAt < 0 ? -1 : time - it.pickedAt;
        const ringMat = it.ring.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
        if (age < 0) {
          it.root.visible = pickupVisible(course.items[i].s, playerS);
          it.model.position.y = 1.3 + Math.sin(time * 2.6 + i) * 0.15;
          it.model.rotation.set(0, time * 1.8 + i, 0);
          it.model.scale.setScalar(1.4);
          const pulse = (time * 0.9 + i * 0.3) % 1;
          it.ring.scale.setScalar(0.8 + pulse * 0.5);
          ringMat.opacity = 0.5 * (1 - pulse);
        } else if (age < POP_TIME) {
          const k = age / POP_TIME;
          it.model.position.y = 1.3 + k * 2.5;
          it.model.scale.setScalar(1.4 * (1 + k * 0.4 - k * k * 1.4));
          ringMat.opacity = 0;
        } else {
          it.root.visible = false;
        }
      });
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { RoundedBoxGeometry } from 'three/examples/jsm/geometries/RoundedBoxGeometry.js';
import { worldAt, type Course } from '../route/course';
import { pickupVisible } from './road';
import { toon } from './scene';

export interface GelsView {
  group: THREE.Group;
  update(time: number, taken: Set<number>, playerS?: number): void;
}

function labelTexture(km: number): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 256;
  c.height = 360;
  const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
  g.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  g.textAlign = 'center';
  g.font = '700 46px Lexend, sans-serif';
  g.fillText('ENERGY', 128, 70);
  g.font = '800 120px Lexend, sans-serif';
  g.fillText('GEL', 128, 190);
  // lightning bolt
  g.beginPath();
  g.moveTo(140, 214);
  g.lineTo(98, 282);
  g.lineTo(126, 282);
  g.lineTo(112, 340);
  g.lineTo(160, 262);
  g.lineTo(132, 262);
  g.closePath();
  g.fill();
  g.font = '600 34px Lexend, sans-serif';
  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
  g.fillText(`${km}K`, 210, 330);
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  return t;
}

/** Soft toy energy-gel pouch: red body, pink shoulders and tear-off cap, white label. */
function pouch(km: number): THREE.Group {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const red = toon('#e8414f');
  const pink = toon('#f4899a');
  const light = toon('#f8a9b4');

  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(1.0, 1.3, 0.32, 4, 0.14), red);
  const band = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(1.02, 0.18, 0.34, 3, 0.08), pink);
  band.position.y = -0.6;
  const shoulder = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(0.62, 0.36, 0.26, 4, 0.12), pink);
  shoulder.position.y = 0.76;
  const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(0.78, 0.2, 0.2, 3, 0.08), light);
  cap.position.y = 0.98;
  g.add(body, band, shoulder, cap);

  const labelMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: labelTexture(km), transparent: true, depthWrite: false });
  for (const side of [1, -1]) {
    const label = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.72, 1.0), labelMat);
    label.position.set(0, 0.02, side * 0.165);
    if (side < 0) label.rotation.y = Math.PI;
    g.add(label);
  }
  g.traverse((o) => {
    if (o instanceof THREE.Mesh && o.material !== labelMat) o.castShadow = true;
  });
  return g;
}

const POP_TIME = 0.4;

/** Big floating gel pouches with a pulsing ground halo. */
export function buildGels(course: Course): GelsView {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const items = course.gels.map((gel) => {
    const p = worldAt(course, gel.s, gel.lateral);
    const root = new THREE.Group();
    root.position.set(p.x, 0, p.z);
    root.rotation.y = Math.atan2(p.tx, p.tz) + Math.PI; // label faces the runner
    const model = pouch(gel.km);
    model.scale.setScalar(1.6);
    const halo = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.RingGeometry(1.4, 2.3, 40),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ff8fa3', transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false }),
    );
    halo.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    halo.position.y = 0.09;
    root.add(model, halo);
    group.add(root);
    return { root, model, halo, pickedAt: -1 };
  });
  let seen = 0;

  return {
    group,
    update(time, taken, playerS) {
      if (taken.size < seen) for (const it of items) it.pickedAt = -1;
      seen = taken.size;
      items.forEach((it, i) => {
        if (taken.has(i) && it.pickedAt < 0) it.pickedAt = time;
        const age = it.pickedAt < 0 ? -1 : time - it.pickedAt;
        const haloMat = it.halo.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
        if (age < 0) {
          it.root.visible = pickupVisible(course.gels[i].s, playerS);
          it.model.position.y = 1.8 + Math.sin(time * 2.4) * 0.18;
          it.model.rotation.set(0, Math.sin(time * 1.6) * 0.5, Math.sin(time * 2.1) * 0.08);
          it.model.scale.setScalar(1.6);
          const pulse = (time * 0.8) % 1;
          it.halo.scale.setScalar(0.8 + pulse * 0.5);
          haloMat.opacity = 0.55 * (1 - pulse);
        } else if (age < POP_TIME) {
          const k = age / POP_TIME;
          it.model.position.y = 1.8 + k * 3;
          it.model.rotation.y += 0.4;
          it.model.scale.setScalar(1.6 * (1 + k * 0.4 - k * k * 1.4));
          haloMat.opacity = 0;
        } else {
          it.root.visible = false;
        }
      });
    },
  };
}
```

8474 bytes · sha256 `ad20f5ab1b96fd32601c06511b6d92d792931b01e2353f932b039fd361ea2eff`

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { ROAD_HALF } from '../game/constants';
import type { Course } from '../route/course';
import { mulberry32 } from '../route/samples';
import { GROUND, toon } from './scene';

/** Grid of course samples for "how far is this point from the road?" queries. */
class RoadGrid {
  private cells = new Map<string, number[]>();
  constructor(private course: Course, private cell = 8) {
    course.samples.forEach((s, i) => {
      const key = `${Math.floor(s.x / cell)},${Math.floor(s.z / cell)}`;
      const list = this.cells.get(key);
      if (list) list.push(i);
      else this.cells.set(key, [i]);
    });
  }
  /** Distance to the road centreline, capped at 2 cells. */
  distance(x: number, z: number): number {
    const cx = Math.floor(x / this.cell);
    const cz = Math.floor(z / this.cell);
    let best = this.cell * 2;
    for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++)
      for (let dz = -2; dz <= 2; dz++) {
        const list = this.cells.get(`${cx + dx},${cz + dz}`);
        if (!list) continue;
        for (const i of list) {
          const s = this.course.samples[i];
          const d = Math.hypot(s.x - x, s.z - z);
          if (d < best) best = d;
        }
      }
    return best;
  }
}

export interface NatureView {
  group: THREE.Group;
  update(time: number): void;
}

const windUniform = { value: 0 };

function grassBlade(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  // tapered 3-segment blade, base at y=0, tip at y=1
  const w = 0.09;
  const pos = [-w, 0, 0, w, 0, 0, -w * 0.75, 0.4, 0, w * 0.75, 0.4, 0, -w * 0.4, 0.75, 0, w * 0.4, 0.75, 0, 0, 1, 0];
  const idx = [0, 1, 2, 2, 1, 3, 2, 3, 4, 4, 3, 5, 4, 5, 6];
  const base = new THREE.Color('#5f9f45');
  const tip = new THREE.Color('#d8f0a0');
  const col: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.length; i += 3) {
    const c = base.clone().lerp(tip, pos[i + 1]);
    col.push(c.r, c.g, c.b);
  }
  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(col, 3));
  // all normals up: blades shade like the ground, which reads as a soft lawn
  g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(new Array((pos.length / 3) * 3).fill(0).map((_, i) => (i % 3 === 1 ? 1 : 0)), 3));
  g.setIndex(idx);
  return g;
}

function windMaterial(): THREE.MeshLambertMaterial {
  const m = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ vertexColors: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
  m.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uWind = windUniform;
    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader
      .replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nuniform float uWind;')
      .replace(
        '#include <begin_vertex>',
        `#include <begin_vertex>
        #ifdef USE_INSTANCING
          vec2 ip = instanceMatrix[3].xz;
          float h = position.y * position.y;
          float sway = sin(uWind * 1.7 + ip.x * 0.12 + ip.y * 0.09) * 0.22 + sin(uWind * 3.3 + ip.x * 0.5) * 0.06;
          transformed.x += sway * h;
          transformed.z += sway * 0.6 * h;
        #endif`,
      );
  };
  return m;
}

export function buildNature(course: Course): NatureView {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const rnd = mulberry32(course.run.id.length * 7919 + course.samples.length);
  const grid = new RoadGrid(course);
  const b = course.bounds;
  const cx = (b.minX + b.maxX) / 2;
  const cz = (b.minZ + b.maxZ) / 2;

  const ground = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1), toon(GROUND));
  ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  ground.scale.set(b.maxX - b.minX + 1600, b.maxZ - b.minZ + 1600, 1);
  ground.position.set(cx, 0, cz);
  ground.receiveShadow = true;
  group.add(ground);

  /** Random point beside the road at a lateral distance in [near, far]; null if it lands on road. */
  const beside = (near: number, far: number, clearance: number) => {
    const s = course.samples[Math.floor(rnd() * course.samples.length)];
    const side = rnd() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
    const off = side * (near + rnd() * (far - near));
    const x = s.x - s.tz * off + (rnd() - 0.5) * 2;
    const z = s.z + s.tx * off + (rnd() - 0.5) * 2;
    return grid.distance(x, z) < clearance ? null : { x, z };
  };

  const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4();
  const q = new THREE.Quaternion();
  const e = new THREE.Euler();
  const v = new THREE.Vector3();
  const sc = new THREE.Vector3();
  const c = new THREE.Color();

  // Grass — dense along the shoulders, swaying in the wind.
  const grassCount = Math.round(course.length * 13);
  const grass = new THREE.InstancedMesh(grassBlade(), windMaterial(), grassCount);
  let gi = 0;
  for (let tries = 0; gi < grassCount && tries < grassCount * 3; tries++) {
    const p = beside(ROAD_HALF + 0.9, ROAD_HALF + 16, ROAD_HALF + 0.9);
    if (!p) continue;
    e.set(0, rnd() * Math.PI, 0);
    const h = 0.5 + rnd() * 0.8;
    m4.compose(v.set(p.x, 0, p.z), q.setFromEuler(e), sc.set(1, h, 1));
    grass.setMatrixAt(gi, m4);
    grass.setColorAt(gi, c.setHSL(0.24 + rnd() * 0.06, 0.45, 0.75 + rnd() * 0.2));
    gi++;
  }
  grass.count = gi;
  group.add(grass);

  // Flowers
  const flowerCount = Math.round(course.length * 1.2);
  const flowers = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.2, 0), toon('#ffffff'), flowerCount);
  const flowerColors = ['#ffffff', '#ffb3c7', '#ffe17a', '#c9b6ff'];
  let fi = 0;
  for (let tries = 0; fi < flowerCount && tries < flowerCount * 3; tries++) {
    const p = beside(ROAD_HALF + 1.2, ROAD_HALF + 12, ROAD_HALF + 1.2);
    if (!p) continue;
    m4.compose(v.set(p.x, 0.35 + rnd() * 0.2, p.z), q.identity(), sc.setScalar(0.7 + rnd() * 0.6));
    flowers.setMatrixAt(fi, m4);
    flowers.setColorAt(fi, c.set(flowerColors[Math.floor(rnd() * flowerColors.length)]));
    fi++;
  }
  flowers.count = fi;
  group.add(flowers);

  // Lollipop trees + round bushes
  const treeCount = Math.round(course.length / 5);
  const trunks = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.32, 2.6, 8).translate(0, 1.3, 0), toon('#b98a62'), treeCount);
  const crowns = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.9, 3), toon('#ffffff'), treeCount);
  const crownColors = ['#7cc26b', '#94d070', '#6db86a', '#f6b8c8', '#ffd38a'];
  let ti = 0;
  for (let tries = 0; ti < treeCount && tries < treeCount * 4; tries++) {
    const p = beside(ROAD_HALF + 5, ROAD_HALF + 70, ROAD_HALF + 5);
    if (!p) continue;
    const s = 0.8 + rnd() * 0.7;
    m4.compose(v.set(p.x, 0, p.z), q.identity(), sc.setScalar(s));
    trunks.setMatrixAt(ti, m4);
    m4.compose(v.set(p.x, 2.9 * s + 1.2 * s, p.z), q.identity(), sc.set(s, s * (0.95 + rnd() * 0.25), s));
    crowns.setMatrixAt(ti, m4);
    crowns.setColorAt(ti, c.set(crownColors[rnd() < 0.8 ? Math.floor(rnd() * 3) : 3 + Math.floor(rnd() * 2)]));
    ti++;
  }
  trunks.count = crowns.count = ti;
  trunks.castShadow = crowns.castShadow = true;
  group.add(trunks, crowns);

  const bushCount = Math.round(course.length / 8);
  const bushes = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 2), toon('#ffffff'), bushCount);
  let bi = 0;
  for (let tries = 0; bi < bushCount && tries < bushCount * 4; tries++) {
    const p = beside(ROAD_HALF + 2.6, ROAD_HALF + 9, ROAD_HALF + 2.6);
    if (!p) continue;
    const s = 0.6 + rnd() * 0.7;
    m4.compose(v.set(p.x, s * 0.45, p.z), q.identity(), sc.set(s * 1.3, s * 0.85, s * 1.2));
    bushes.setMatrixAt(bi, m4);
    bushes.setColorAt(bi, c.set('#6fb35a').offsetHSL(rnd() * 0.04, 0, rnd() * 0.08));
    bi++;
  }
  bushes.count = bi;
  bushes.castShadow = true;
  group.add(bushes);

  // Puffy clouds — no fog so they stay white against the sky.
  const cloudMat = toon('#ffffff', { fog: false, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95 });
  const puff = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 2);
  const span = Math.max(b.maxX - b.minX, b.maxZ - b.minZ) + 400;
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    const cloud = new THREE.Group();
    const parts = 4 + Math.floor(rnd() * 4);
    for (let k = 0; k < parts; k++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(puff, cloudMat);
      const r = 8 + rnd() * 10;
      m.scale.set(r, r * 0.7, r);
      m.position.set((k - parts / 2) * 9 + rnd() * 4, rnd() * 5, rnd() * 8);
      cloud.add(m);
    }
    cloud.position.set(cx + (rnd() - 0.5) * span, 85 + rnd() * 50, cz + (rnd() - 0.5) * span);
    cloud.rotation.y = rnd() * Math.PI;
    group.add(cloud);
  }

  return {
    group,
    update(time) {
      windUniform.value = time;
    },
  };
}
```
